- [ ] **Passo 3: Os testes do builder**

Criar `test/ir/builder_test.dart`:

```dart
// buildSection (spec do IR §9): cada linha da tabela de §9.1 e os casos de
// §9.2–§9.12, pelo caminho inteiro (parse, folhas, cascata, construção),
// sempre com checkSection.
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/css/cascade.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/css/loader.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/diagnostics/diagnostic.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/builder.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/foreign.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/model.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/xhtml_parse.dart';

import 'support/ir_fixtures.dart';

Future<Section> _s(
  String body, {
  String head = '',
  Map<String, Object?> files = const {},
}) async {
  final b = await buildXhtml(goldenDoc(body, head: head), files: files);
  expect(checkSection(b.section), isNull);
  return b.section;
}

Future<Built> _b(String body, {int budget = irBuildBudget}) async {
  final b = await buildXhtml(goldenDoc(body), budget: budget);
  expect(checkSection(b.section), isNull);
  return b;
}

List<BlockKind> _kinds(Section s) => [for (final b in s.leaves) b.kind];

List<String> _texts(Section s) => [
  for (final b in s.leaves) s.canonicalText.substring(b.textStart, b.textEnd),
];

Iterable<EpubDiagnostic> _diag(Section s, EpubDiagnosticCode c) =>
    s.diagnostics.where((d) => identical(d.code, c));

void main() {
  group('§9.1 mapeamento', () {
    test('head, title, script, style e template saem pelo tipo', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p>a<script>x()</script><style>p{}</style><template><p>t</p></template>'
        '<title>no corpo</title>b</p>',
      );
      expect(s.canonicalText, 'ab\n');
    });

    test('script e style além da profundidade 256 também saem', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '${'<span>' * 300}a<script>x()</script><style>s</style>b'
        '${'</span>' * 300}',
      );
      expect(s.canonicalText, 'ab\n');
    });

    test(
      'profundidade > 256: transparente — texto e âncoras, sem bloco nem link',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '${'<div>' * 260}<p id="fundo">x<a href="c.xhtml">y</a></p>'
          '<blockquote>z</blockquote>${'</div>' * 260}',
        );
        expect(s.canonicalText, 'xyz\n');
        expect(s.blocks, hasLength(1));
        expect(s.links, isEmpty);
        expect(s.anchors['fundo'], 0);
      },
    );

    test('profundidade 256 tem estrutura; 257 não', () async {
      Future<Section> at(int depth) => _s(
        '${'<div>' * (depth - 3)}<p>a</p><p>b</p>${'</div>' * (depth - 3)}',
      );
      // html (1) > body (2) > div × (depth − 3) > p: o p fica em depth.
      expect((await at(256)).leaves, hasLength(2));
      expect((await at(257)).leaves, hasLength(1));
    });

    test('hr de bloco: sceneBreak, sem conteúdo; br e wbr', () async {
      final s = await _s('<p>a<br/>b<wbr/>c</p><hr/><p>d</p>');
      expect(s.canonicalText, 'a\u{2028}b\u{200B}c\n\nd\n');
      expect(_kinds(s), [
        BlockKind.paragraph,
        BlockKind.sceneBreak,
        BlockKind.paragraph,
      ]);
    });

    test('col e colgroup ignorados; object e video transparentes', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<table><colgroup><col/></colgroup><tr><td>a</td></tr></table>'
        '<p><video>sem vídeo</video> <object>sem objeto</object></p>',
      );
      expect(s.canonicalText, 'a\nsem vídeo sem objeto\n');
      expect(s.leaves.last.objects, isEmpty);
    });

    test('verso: dentro de z3998:poem ou z3998:verse, paragraph e code viram verse', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<div epub:type="z3998:poem"><p>linha</p><pre>pre</pre></div>'
        '<p role="z3998:verse">outro</p><p>prosa</p>',
      );
      expect(_kinds(s), [
        BlockKind.verse,
        BlockKind.verse,
        BlockKind.verse,
        BlockKind.paragraph,
      ]);
    });

    test('code de bloco: pre e code/kbd/samp/tt com display block', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<pre>a</pre><code style="display:block">b</code><p><code>c</code></p>',
      );
      expect(_kinds(s), [BlockKind.code, BlockKind.code, BlockKind.paragraph]);
      expect(s.leaves.last.runs.first.attrs, InlineAttr.code);
      expect(s.leaves.toList()[1].runs.first.attrs, InlineAttr.code);
    });

    test('titleLike, align e heading', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<h1 epub:type="title">T</h1><h2 style="text-align:center">C</h2>'
        '<p role="doc-subtitle">s</p><p epub:type="dedication">d</p><p>p</p>',
      );
      final l = s.leaves.toList();
      expect(l.map((b) => (b.kind, b.level)), [
        (BlockKind.heading, 1),
        (BlockKind.heading, 2),
        (BlockKind.paragraph, 0),
        (BlockKind.paragraph, 0),
        (BlockKind.paragraph, 0),
      ]);
      expect(l.map((b) => b.titleLike), [true, false, true, true, false]);
      expect(l[1].align, BlockAlign.center);
      expect(l[0].runs.first.attrs & InlineAttr.sizeLarger, isNot(0));
    });

    test('epub:switch sem xmlns:epub: só o default', () async {
      final b = await buildXhtml(
        '<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml"><body>'
        '<epub:switch><epub:case><p>A</p></epub:case>'
        '<epub:default><p>B</p></epub:default><epub:default><p>C</p>'
        '</epub:default></epub:switch></body></html>',
      );
      expect(b.text, 'B\n');
    });
  });

  group('§9.2 blocos, folhas e achatamento', () {
    test('folha anônima antes, entre e depois de blocos (#19)', () async {
      final s = await _s('<div>a<p>b</p>c<p>d</p>e</div>');
      expect(_texts(s), ['a\n', 'b\n', 'c\n', 'd\n', 'e\n']);
      expect(s.blocks, hasLength(5), reason: 'div achatado');
    });

    test('inline entre filhos de bloco em cada container', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<blockquote>a<p>b</p></blockquote><figure>c<p>d</p></figure>'
        '<aside>e<p>f</p></aside><ul>g<li>h</li></ul>'
        '<table>i<tr>j<td>k</td></tr></table>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.map((b) => b.container), [
        ContainerKind.blockquote,
        ContainerKind.figure,
        ContainerKind.aside,
        ContainerKind.list,
        ContainerKind.table,
      ]);
      final list = s.blocks[3].children!;
      expect(list.map((b) => (b.kind, b.marker)), [
        (BlockKind.paragraph, null),
        (BlockKind.listItem, ListMarker.disc),
      ]);
      final table = s.blocks[4].children!;
      expect(table.map((b) => b.container), [
        ContainerKind.tableRow,
        ContainerKind.tableRow,
      ]);
      expect(table[1].children!.map((b) => b.kind), [
        BlockKind.tableCell,
        BlockKind.tableCell,
      ]);
      expect(s.canonicalText, 'a\nb\nc\nd\ne\nf\ng\nh\ni\nj\nk\n');
    });

    test('li e td com blocos viram blocos com children', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<ul><li>a<p>b</p></li></ul><table><tr><td><p>x</p><p>y</p></td></tr></table>',
      );
      final li = s.blocks[0].children!.single;
      expect((li.kind, li.isLeaf), (BlockKind.listItem, false));
      expect(li.children!.map((b) => b.kind), [
        BlockKind.paragraph,
        BlockKind.paragraph,
      ]);
      expect((li.marker, li.ordinal), (ListMarker.disc, 1));
      final td = s.blocks[1].children!.single.children!.single;
      expect((td.kind, td.children!.length), (BlockKind.tableCell, 2));
    });

    test('vazios: p some (ids vão ao próximo); li e td ficam com \\n; list, table e tr somem (#20)', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p id="v"></p><p> </p><ul></ul><table><tr></tr></table>'
        '<ul><li id="i"></li></ul><table><tr><td></td></tr></table><p>z</p>',
      );
      expect(s.canonicalText, '\n\nz\n');
      expect(s.blocks.map((b) => b.container), [
        ContainerKind.list,
        ContainerKind.table,
        null,
      ]);
      expect(s.anchors, {'v': 2, 'i': 2});
    });

    test(
      'break-before vai ao primeiro bloco; break-after ao último (#22)',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '<div style="break-before:page;break-after:page"><p>a</p><p>b</p></div><p>c</p>',
        );
        expect(s.leaves.map((b) => (b.breakBefore, b.breakAfter)), [
          (BreakHint.page, BreakHint.auto),
          (BreakHint.auto, BreakHint.page),
          (BreakHint.auto, BreakHint.auto),
        ]);
      },
    );

    test(
      'pai e filho na mesma fronteira: vale o mais forte (Fragmentation §3.1)',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '<div style="break-before:avoid"><p style="page-break-before:always">a</p></div>'
          '<div style="break-after:page"><p style="break-after:avoid">b</p></div>',
        );
        expect(s.leaves.map((b) => (b.breakBefore, b.breakAfter)), [
          (BreakHint.page, BreakHint.auto),
          (BreakHint.auto, BreakHint.page),
        ]);
      },
    );

    test('break de elemento que não emite bloco vai ao próximo; no fim, some (#45)', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p>a</p><div style="break-after:page"></div><p>b</p><div style="break-before:page"></div>',
      );
      expect(s.leaves.map((b) => b.breakBefore), [
        BreakHint.auto,
        BreakHint.page,
      ]);
    });

    test(
      'break-inside: avoid vira generic; com um filho só, o filho sobe',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '<div style="break-inside:avoid"><p>a</p><p>b</p></div>'
          '<div style="page-break-inside:avoid"><p>c</p></div>'
          '<p style="break-inside:avoid">d</p>',
        );
        expect(s.blocks.map((b) => (b.container, b.breakInside)), [
          (ContainerKind.generic, BreakHint.avoid),
          (null, BreakHint.avoid),
          (null, BreakHint.avoid),
        ]);
      },
    );

    test('container só com inline ganha uma folha anônima', () async {
      final s = await _s('<blockquote>só texto</blockquote>');
      final q = s.blocks.single;
      expect(q.container, ContainerKind.blockquote);
      expect(q.children!.single.kind, BlockKind.paragraph);
    });
  });

  group('§9.3 listas', () {
    test('start, value, reversed com e sem start', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<ol reversed=""><li>a</li><li>b</li><li>c</li></ol>'
        '<ol start=" 3x"><li>a</li><li value="9">b</li><li>c</li></ol>'
        '<ol reversed="" start="2"><li>a</li><li>b</li><li>c</li></ol>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.map((b) => (b.start, b.reversed)), [
        (3, true),
        (3, false),
        (2, true),
      ]);
      expect(s.blocks.map((b) => [for (final c in b.children!) c.ordinal]), [
        [3, 2, 1],
        [3, 9, 10],
        [2, 1, 0],
      ]);
    });

    test('ul, menu e dir: start ignorado (null), contam de 1', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<ul start="5" reversed=""><li>a</li><li>b</li></ul><menu><li>c</li></menu>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.map((b) => (b.start, b.reversed)), [
        (null, false),
        (null, false),
      ]);
      expect([for (final c in s.blocks[0].children!) c.ordinal], [1, 2]);
    });

    test('item fora de lista; display: list-item em p; type do ol', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<li>solto</li><li value="4">v</li><p style="display:list-item">p</p>'
        '<ol type="a"><li>x</li></ol>',
      );
      final l = s.leaves.toList();
      expect(l.map((b) => (b.kind, b.ordinal, b.level)), [
        (BlockKind.listItem, 1, 1),
        (BlockKind.listItem, 4, 1),
        (BlockKind.listItem, 1, 1),
        (BlockKind.listItem, 1, 1),
      ]);
      expect(l.last.marker, ListMarker.lowerAlpha);
    });

    test('li com display que não é list-item não tem marcador', () async {
      final s = await _s('<ul><li style="display:block">a</li></ul>');
      expect(s.blocks.single.children!.single.kind, BlockKind.paragraph);
    });

    test('ordinal saturado em ±(2^31 − 1) (#46)', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<ol start="2147483646"><li>a</li><li>b</li><li>c</li></ol>'
        '<ol reversed="" start="-2147483646"><li>a</li><li>b</li><li>c</li></ol>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.map((b) => [for (final c in b.children!) c.ordinal]), [
        [2147483646, 2147483647, 2147483647],
        [-2147483646, -2147483647, -2147483647],
      ]);
    });

    test('níveis aninhados e marcadores herdados', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<ul><li>1<ul><li>2<ul><li>3<ul><li>4</li></ul></li></ul></li></ul></li></ul>',
      );
      final items = <Block>[];
      void collect(Block b) {
        if (b.kind == BlockKind.listItem) items.add(b);
        for (final c in b.children ?? const <Block>[]) {
          collect(c);
        }
      }

      s.blocks.forEach(collect);
      expect(items.map((b) => (b.level, b.marker)), [
        (1, ListMarker.disc),
        (2, ListMarker.circle),
        (3, ListMarker.square),
        (4, ListMarker.square),
      ]);
    });
  });

  group('§9.4 tabelas', () {
    test(
      'linha e célula implícitas; thead marca isHeaderRow; th isHeaderCell',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '<table><thead><tr><th>h</th></tr></thead><td>a</td><td>b</td>'
          '<tr>solto<td>c</td></tr></table>',
        );
        final rows = s.blocks.single.children!;
        expect(rows.map((r) => (r.isHeaderRow, r.children!.length)), [
          (true, 1),
          (false, 2),
          (false, 2),
        ]);
        expect(rows[0].children!.single.isHeaderCell, isTrue);
        expect(s.canonicalText, 'h\na\nb\nsolto\nc\n');
      },
    );

    test('colspan e rowspan: 1000 e 1001, 0, inválido', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<table><tr><td colspan="1000" rowspan="1001">a</td><td colspan="0">b</td>'
        '<td rowspan="x">c</td></tr></table>',
      );
      final cells = s.leaves.toList();
      expect(cells.map((c) => (c.colSpan, c.rowSpan)), [
        (1000, 1000),
        (1, 1),
        (1, 1),
      ]);
    });

    test('tabela dentro de célula; caption', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<table><caption>cap</caption><tr><td><table><tr><td>x</td></tr></table>'
        '</td></tr></table>',
      );
      final t = s.blocks.single;
      expect(t.children!.first.kind, BlockKind.caption);
      final cell = t.children![1].children!.single;
      expect(cell.children!.single.container, ContainerKind.table);
    });

    test(
      'papéis só com o ancestral certo (#44): td em div e tr solto',
      () async {
        final s = await _s('<div><td>a</td></div><tr><th>b</th></tr>');
        expect(_kinds(s), [BlockKind.paragraph, BlockKind.paragraph]);
        expect(s.leaves.every((b) => !b.isHeaderCell), isTrue);
      },
    );
  });

  group('§9.5 notas e links', () {
    test('AsideKind por epub:type e role; o tipo vence o role', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<aside>a</aside><aside epub:type="footnote">b</aside>'
        '<section epub:type="endnote">c</section><div epub:type="rearnote">d</div>'
        '<div role="doc-footnote">e</div><section role="doc-endnote">f</section>'
        '<aside epub:type="endnote" role="doc-footnote">g</aside>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.map((b) => b.aside), [
        AsideKind.aside,
        AsideKind.footnote,
        AsideKind.endnote,
        AsideKind.rearnote,
        AsideKind.footnote,
        AsideKind.endnote,
        AsideKind.endnote,
      ]);
    });

    test('noteref interno vira noteRef; externo fica link', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p><a epub:type="noteref" href="#n1">1</a><a role="doc-noteref" href="n.xhtml#n2">2</a>'
        '<a epub:type="noteref" href="http://x.org">3</a></p>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.single.runs.map((r) => (r.attrs, r.linkIndex)), [
        (InlineAttr.noteRef, 0),
        (InlineAttr.noteRef, 1),
        (InlineAttr.link, 2),
        (0, -1),
      ]);
      expect(
        s.links[0],
        const LinkTarget(href: goldenPath, fragment: 'n1', isExternal: false),
      );
      expect(s.links[2].isExternal, isTrue);
    });

    test(
      'esquemas recusados e travessia ficam sem alvo; dedupe de alvos',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '<p><a href="javascript:x()">j</a><a href="../../../x.xhtml">t</a>'
          '<a href="c2.xhtml">a</a><a href=" c2.xhtml ">b</a><a href="c2.xhtml#x">c</a></p>',
        );
        expect(s.links, hasLength(2));
        expect(s.blocks.single.runs.map((r) => r.linkIndex), [-1, 0, 1, -1]);
      },
    );

    test('a dentro de a: vale o mais interno, inclusive sem alvo', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p><a href="a.xhtml">x<a href="b.xhtml">y</a><a href="data:x">z</a></a></p>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.single.runs.map((r) => r.linkIndex), [0, 1, -1]);
    });

    test('href de 4 096 tem alvo; 4 097 não', () async {
      final ok = 'a' * (4096 - 6);
      final s = await _s(
        '<p><a href="$ok.xhtml">x</a><a href="a$ok.xhtml">y</a></p>',
      );
      expect(s.links, hasLength(1));
    });
  });

  group('§9.6 imagens', () {
    test('alt ausente e vazio; dimensões; remota; de bloco', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p>a<img src="i.png"/><img src="i.png" alt="" width="30" height="40%"/>'
        '<img src="http://x.org/i.png" alt="  r  "/></p>'
        '<p><img src="b.png" style="display:block" alt="B"/>x</p>',
      );
      final objs = s.leaves.first.objects;
      expect(
        objs.map((o) => (o.alt, o.intrinsicWidth, o.intrinsicHeight, o.href)),
        [
          (null, null, null, 'OEBPS/Text/i.png'),
          ('', 30.0, null, 'OEBPS/Text/i.png'),
          ('r', null, null, 'http://x.org/i.png'),
        ],
      );
      expect(_kinds(s), [
        BlockKind.paragraph,
        BlockKind.object,
        BlockKind.paragraph,
      ]);
    });

    test('img sozinha num li continua listItem', () async {
      final s = await _s('<ul><li><img src="i.png"/></li></ul>');
      final li = s.blocks.single.children!.single;
      expect(
        (li.kind, li.object, li.objects.length),
        (BlockKind.listItem, null, 1),
      );
    });
  });

  group('§9.7 SVG', () {
    const ns =
        'xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink"';

    test('invólucro com href e com title: imagem; alt do title', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<div><svg $ns width="5" height="6"><title>Capa</title><image href="c.jpg"/></svg></div>',
      );
      final o = s.blocks.single.object!;
      expect(
        (o.kind, o.href, o.alt, o.intrinsicWidth, o.svgMarkup),
        (ObjectKind.image, 'OEBPS/Text/c.jpg', 'Capa', 5.0, null),
      );
    });

    test('dois image: objeto svg com markup; alt do <text>', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p>x<svg $ns viewBox="0 0 2 3"><image href="a"/><image href="b"/>'
        '<text>rótulo</text></svg></p>',
      );
      final o = s.blocks.single.objects.single;
      expect(
        (o.kind, o.href, o.alt, o.intrinsicWidth),
        (ObjectKind.svg, '', 'rótulo', 2.0),
      );
      expect(
        o.svgMarkup,
        startsWith('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"'),
      );
    });

    test('ids dentro do svg apontam para o U+FFFC', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p>ab<svg $ns><g id="g1"><rect id="r"/></g></svg></p>',
      );
      expect(s.anchors, {'g1': 2, 'r': 2});
    });

    test('markup de 1 Mi fica; 1 Mi + 1 é null', () async {
      final probe = await _s('<p><svg $ns><text>x</text></svg></p>');
      final overhead = probe.blocks.single.object!.svgMarkup!.length - 1;
      final ok = await _s(
        '<p><svg $ns><text>${'x' * (maxSvgMarkupLength - overhead)}</text></svg></p>',
      );
      expect(ok.blocks.single.object!.svgMarkup!.length, maxSvgMarkupLength);
      final over = await _s(
        '<p><svg $ns><text>${'x' * (maxSvgMarkupLength - overhead + 1)}</text></svg></p>',
      );
      expect(over.blocks.single.object!.svgMarkup, isNull);
    });
  });

  group('§9.8 ruby e MathML', () {
    test('rtc com um rt, texto direto em rtc, espaço entre rp e rt', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p><ruby>a<rtc><rt>b</rt></rtc></ruby> <ruby>c<rtc>d</rtc></ruby> '
        '<ruby>e<rp>(</rp> <rt>f</rt> <rp>)</rp></ruby></p>',
      );
      expect(s.canonicalText, 'a(b) c(d) e( f )\n');
      expect(_diag(s, EpubDiagnosticCode.rubyFlattened), hasLength(1));
    });

    test('MathML: tokens, texto fora de token, annotation-xml; display=block', () async {
      const m = 'xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"';
      final s = await _s(
        '<p>a <math $m>solto<mi>x</mi><annotation-xml><mi>y</mi></annotation-xml></math> b'
        '<math $m display="block" alttext="E=mc2"/>c</p>',
      );
      expect(_texts(s), ['a x b\n', 'E=mc2\n', 'c\n']);
      expect(
        _diag(s, EpubDiagnosticCode.unsupportedMath).single.severity,
        EpubSeverity.info,
      );
    });
  });

  group('§9.9 âncoras', () {
    test('vazio, escondido, a[name] depois de id, repetidos', () async {
      final b = await _b(
        '<p id="a">x<span id="b"/><span style="display:none"><b id="c">oculto</b></span>y</p>'
        '<a name="d"/><p id="d">z</p><a name="e"/><a name="e"/><p id="a">w</p>',
      );
      expect(b.section.anchors, {'a': 0, 'b': 1, 'c': 1, 'd': 3, 'e': 5});
      expect(b.result.duplicateAnchors, 1);
      expect(b.result.duplicateSample, 'a');
    });

    test('id de 1 024 fica; de 1 025 é truncado em 1 024', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p id="${'i' * 1024}">a</p><p id="${'j' * 1025}">b</p>',
      );
      expect(s.anchors.keys.map((k) => k.length), [1024, 1024]);
      expect(s.anchors.keys.last, 'j' * 1024);
    });

    test('âncora no fim: último caractere; seção vazia: 0', () async {
      expect((await _s('<p>ab</p><span id="z"/>')).anchors, {'z': 2});
      expect((await _s('<span id="z"/>')).anchors, {'z': 0});
    });
  });

  group('§9.10 lang e dir', () {
    test('xml:lang vence lang; lang vazio; 64 e 65', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p xml:lang="en" lang="fr">a</p><p lang="">b</p><p lang="${'x' * 64}">c</p>'
        '<p lang="${'y' * 65}">d</p><p lang="pt">e</p>',
      );
      expect(s.leaves.map((b) => b.lang), ['en', '', 'x' * 64, null, null]);
    });

    test('Section.dir do body; Block.dir só quando difere', () async {
      final b = await buildXhtml(
        '<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml"><body dir="rtl">'
        '<p>a</p><p dir="ltr">b</p></body></html>',
      );
      expect(b.section.dir, IrDirection.rtl);
      expect(b.section.leaves.map((x) => x.dir), [null, IrDirection.ltr]);
      expect(b.section.lang, isNull);
    });
  });

  group('§9.11 atributos inline', () {
    test('passo de tamanho satura em ±4; small dentro de big anula', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p>${'<big>' * 5}a${'</big>' * 5}<big><small>b</small></big>'
        '${'<small>' * 6}c${'</small>' * 6}</p>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.single.runs.map((r) => r.attrs), [
        InlineAttr.sizeLarger,
        0,
        InlineAttr.sizeSmaller,
        0,
      ]);
    });

    test(
      'ênfase, negrito, sup/sub, versalete, sublinhado, riscado, caixa',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '<p><i>a</i><b>b</b><sup>c</sup><sub>d</sub><span style="font-variant:small-caps">e</span>'
          '<u>f</u><s>g</s><span style="text-transform:lowercase">h</span>'
          '<span style="text-transform:capitalize">i</span></p>',
        );
        expect(s.blocks.single.runs.map((r) => r.attrs).toList(), [
          InlineAttr.emphasis,
          InlineAttr.strong,
          InlineAttr.superscript | InlineAttr.sizeSmaller,
          InlineAttr.subscript | InlineAttr.sizeSmaller,
          InlineAttr.smallCaps,
          InlineAttr.underline,
          InlineAttr.strikethrough,
          InlineAttr.lowercase,
          InlineAttr.capitalize,
          0,
        ]);
      },
    );
  });

  group('§9.12 tetos e orçamento', () {
    test('texto exatamente no teto não corta; um acima corta', () async {
      final exact = await _b('<p>${'a' * (maxSectionTextLength - 1)}</p>');
      expect(exact.section.totalChars, maxSectionTextLength);
      expect(_diag(exact.section, EpubDiagnosticCode.sectionTooLarge), isEmpty);
      final over = await _b(
        '<p>${'a' * (maxSectionTextLength - 1)}</p><p id="x">b</p>',
      );
      expect(over.section.totalChars, maxSectionTextLength);
      final d = _diag(over.section, EpubDiagnosticCode.sectionTooLarge).single;
      expect(d.details['reason'], 'text');
      expect(d.details['limit'], maxSectionTextLength);
      expect(over.section.anchors['x'], maxSectionTextLength - 1);
      final cut = await _b('<p>${'a' * maxSectionTextLength}</p>');
      expect(cut.section.totalChars, maxSectionTextLength);
      expect(cut.text.endsWith('a\n'), isTrue);
    });

    test('200 000 blocos cabem; o 200 001º corta e o resto vira placeholder', () async {
      final ok = await _b('<p>x</p>' * maxSectionBlocks);
      expect(ok.section.blocks, hasLength(maxSectionBlocks));
      expect(ok.result.blocksTruncated, isFalse);
      final cut = await _b(
        '${'<p>x</p>' * maxSectionBlocks}<p id="r">resto</p><p id="m">mais</p>',
      );
      expect(cut.result.blocksTruncated, isTrue);
      final blocks = cut.section.blocks;
      expect(blocks, hasLength(maxSectionBlocks + 1));
      final rest = blocks.last;
      expect(
        (rest.kind, rest.placeholder, rest.placeholderDetail),
        (BlockKind.placeholder, PlaceholderReason.truncated, goldenPath),
      );
      expect(cut.text.substring(rest.textStart), 'resto mais\n');
      expect(
        cut.section.anchors.containsKey('m'),
        isFalse,
        reason: 'id do resto',
      );
      expect(
        cut.section.anchors['r'],
        rest.textStart,
        reason: 'pendente: vai ao primeiro caractere do placeholder',
      );
    });

    test('orçamento injetado: corta, pendentes vão ao placeholder, nada some', () async {
      const body =
          '<div><p>um</p><p id="dois">dois</p><ul><li>três</li></ul><p>quatro</p></div>';
      final full = await _b(body);
      expect(full.result.budgetExhausted, isFalse);
      // 12 passos: a caminhada para no texto "dois", com o id já registrado.
      final b = await _b(body, budget: 12);
      expect(b.result.budgetExhausted, isTrue);
      expect(b.text, 'um\ndois três quatro\n');
      expect(b.section.blocks.map((x) => x.kind), [
        BlockKind.paragraph,
        BlockKind.placeholder,
      ]);
      expect(b.section.anchors, {'dois': 3});
      // 11: para antes de entrar no p, e o id do resto não entra.
      expect((await _b(body, budget: 11)).section.anchors, isEmpty);
    });

    test('as passadas do svg pagam o orçamento', () async {
      final b = await _b(
        '<p>a</p><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">${'<g>' * 2000}${'</g>' * 2000}</svg><p>b</p>',
        budget: 1500,
      );
      expect(b.result.budgetExhausted, isTrue);
      expect(b.text, endsWith('b\n'));
    });
  });

  group('foco de revisão', () {
    test(
      'xml:lang só no body: Section.lang null, folhas com o lang do body',
      () async {
        final b = await buildXhtml(
          '<html xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml"><body xml:lang="pt">'
          '<p>a</p><p lang="en">b</p></body></html>',
        );
        expect(b.section.lang, isNull);
        expect(b.section.leaves.map((x) => x.lang), ['pt', 'en']);
      },
    );

    test(
      'href vazio ou só espaço: sem link; # sozinho: interno sem fragmento',
      () async {
        final s = await _s(
          '<p><a href="">a</a><a href="  ">b</a><a href="#">c</a></p>',
        );
        expect(s.links, const [
          LinkTarget(href: goldenPath, isExternal: false),
        ]);
        expect(s.blocks.single.runs.map((r) => (r.attrs, r.linkIndex)), [
          (0, -1),
          (InlineAttr.link, 0),
          (0, -1),
        ]);
      },
    );

    test('src e href com %20: decodificados pelo decodePath', () async {
      final s = await _s(
        '<p><img src="../Images/capa%20nova.png"/><a href="cap%202.xhtml#n">x</a></p>',
      );
      expect(s.blocks.single.objects.single.href, 'OEBPS/Images/capa nova.png');
      expect(s.links.single.href, 'OEBPS/Text/cap 2.xhtml');
    });

    test('<p><br/></p> e <p>&#xA0;</p>: folhas que não somem', () async {
      final s = await _s('<p><br/></p><p>&#xA0;</p><p> </p>');
      expect(_texts(s), ['\u{2028}\n', '\u{00A0}\n']);
    });
  });

  group('cessão', () {
    int yields(String body) {
      final parsed = ParseResult();
      for (final _ in parseXhtml(
        goldenDoc(body),
        sectionPath: goldenPath,
        html: false,
        sink: DiagnosticSink(),
        into: parsed,
      )) {}
      final document = parsed.parsed.document;
      final styles = computeStylesSync(
        document,
        SectionSheets.empty,
        sectionPath: goldenPath,
        sink: DiagnosticSink(),
      );
      var n = 0;
      for (final _ in buildSection(
        document,
        styles,
        sectionPath: goldenPath,
        sink: DiagnosticSink(),
        into: BuildResult(),
      )) {
        n++;
      }
      return n;
    }

    test('cede a cada folha fechada', () {
      expect(yields('<p>a</p>' * 10), 10);
    });

    test('cede a cada 64 Ki de texto, inclusive dentro de uma folha', () {
      // 1 Mi num nó só: 16 fatias de escrita e as fatias da NFC.
      expect(yields('<p>${'c ' * (512 * 1024)}</p>'), greaterThanOrEqualTo(32));
      // 1 Mi em 1 000 nós de 1 Ki: a cada 64 Ki escritos.
      expect(
        yields('<p>${'<b>${'d' * 1024}</b>' * 1000}</p>'),
        greaterThanOrEqualTo(15),
      );
    });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: Os hostis da construção**

Criar `test/ir/ir_hostile_test.dart`:

```dart
// Hostis da construção (spec do IR §15.4): cada um pelo caminho inteiro
// (parse, folhas, cascata, construção), com teto de 5 s sobre a construção
// e a afirmação de corte certo. Os do parse ficam em xhtml_parse_test e em
// tolerant_parse_test; o de 17 MiB, em section_reader_test; o do EPIR, em
// codec_test.
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/builder.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/model.dart';

import 'support/ir_fixtures.dart';

/// Constrói [body]; o teto de 5 s vale só para a construção (o parse e a
/// cascata têm os hostis deles).
Future<Built> _timed(String body, {bool tolerant = false}) async {
  final watch = Stopwatch();
  final b = await buildXhtml(
    goldenDoc(body, tolerant: tolerant),
    buildWatch: watch,
  );
  expect(watch.elapsed, lessThan(const Duration(seconds: 5)));
  expect(checkSection(b.section), isNull);
  return b;
}

void main() {
  test('100 000 níveis de div, nos dois caminhos: 256 com estrutura', () async {
    for (final tolerant in [false, true]) {
      final b = await _timed(
        '${'<div>' * 100000}<p>fundo</p>${'</div>' * 100000}',
        tolerant: tolerant,
      );
      expect(b.text, 'fundo\n');
    }
  });

  test('1 Mi de texto com espaços e LFs alternados, num p e num pre', () async {
    final p = await _timed('<p>${'a \n' * (350 * 1024)}</p>');
    expect(p.section.blocks, hasLength(1));
    final pre = await _timed('<pre>${'a \n' * (350 * 1024)}</pre>');
    expect(pre.section.blocks.single.kind, BlockKind.code);
  });

  test(
    '1 Mi de e + U+0301 e uma base com 100 000 marcas: NFC linear',
    () async {
      final alt = await _timed('<p>${'e\u{0301}' * (512 * 1024)}</p>');
      expect(alt.text.length, 512 * 1024 + 1);
      final marks = await _timed('<p>a${'\u{0301}' * 100000}</p>');
      expect(marks.text.length, 100001);
    },
  );

  test(
    '100 000 marcas, cada uma num b e num i alternados: um trecho NFC',
    () async {
      final body = StringBuffer('<p>e');
      for (var i = 0; i < 100000; i++) {
        body.write(i.isEven ? '<b>\u{0301}</b>' : '<i>\u{0301}</i>');
      }
      body.write('</p>');
      final b = await _timed(body.toString());
      expect(b.text, '\u{00E9}${'\u{0301}' * 99999}\n');
      expect(b.section.blocks.single.runs, hasLength(1));
    },
  );

  test(
    '250 000 <p>x</p>: sectionTruncated blocks em 200 000, o resto no topo',
    () async {
      final b = await _timed('<p>x</p>' * 250000);
      expect(b.result.blocksTruncated, isTrue);
      final rest = b.section.blocks.skip(maxSectionBlocks).toList();
      expect(rest, hasLength(7), reason: '100 000 unidades em folhas de 16 Ki');
      expect(
        rest.every((r) => r.placeholder == PlaceholderReason.truncated),
        isTrue,
      );
      expect(b.text.length, maxSectionBlocks * 2 + 50000 * 2);
    },
  );

  test(
    '3 Mi de texto: sectionTooLarge text, totalChars exatamente 2 Mi',
    () async {
      final b = await _timed('<p>${'palavra ' * (384 * 1024)}</p>');
      expect(b.section.totalChars, maxSectionTextLength);
    },
  );

  test('100 000 id iguais e 10 000 distintos de 1 024 unidades', () async {
    final same = await _timed('<p id="x">a</p>' * 100000);
    expect(same.result.duplicateAnchors, 99999);
    final distinct = StringBuffer();
    for (var i = 0; i < 10000; i++) {
      final id = '$i'.padLeft(1024, 'i');
      distinct.write('<p id="$id">a</p>');
    }
    final d = await _timed(distinct.toString());
    expect(d.section.anchors, hasLength(10000));
  });

  test(
    '100 000 links para o mesmo alvo e para alvos distintos; href de 1 Mi',
    () async {
      final same = await _timed(
        '<p>${'<a href="c.xhtml#n">x</a>' * 100000}</p>',
      );
      expect(same.section.links, hasLength(1));
      final distinct = StringBuffer('<p>');
      for (var i = 0; i < 100000; i++) {
        distinct.write('<a href="c.xhtml#n$i">x</a>');
      }
      distinct.write('</p>');
      expect(
        (await _timed(distinct.toString())).section.links,
        hasLength(100000),
      );
      final long = await _timed('<p><a href="${'a' * (1 << 20)}">x</a></p>');
      expect(long.section.links, isEmpty);
    },
  );

  test('svg de 2 Mi: svgMarkup null', () async {
    final b = await _timed(
      '<p><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">${'<rect width="1"/>' * 120000}</svg></p>',
    );
    expect(b.section.blocks.single.object!.svgMarkup, isNull);
  });

  test('ol reversed com 100 000 li: uma contagem', () async {
    final b = await _timed('<ol reversed="">${'<li>x</li>' * 100000}</ol>');
    final items = b.section.blocks.single.children!;
    expect((items.first.ordinal, items.last.ordinal), (100000, 1));
  });
}
```
