- [ ] **Passo 6: Implementar**

Criar `lib/src/ir/builder.dart`:

```dart
/// Construção da IR (spec do IR §9): uma caminhada com pilha explícita sobre
/// os `nodes` do DOM, a partir da raiz, com o estilo computado da cascata.
/// Produz o texto canônico (§8), os blocos planos com containers rasos, os
/// runs, os links, os objetos e as âncoras de uma seção.
///
/// Por que é linear:
/// - uma caminhada com pilha explícita; cada nó é visitado uma vez; a
///   subárvore escondida, a de `svg` e a de `math` são percorridas uma vez
///   cada pelas passadas extras e não de novo pela caminhada;
/// - as passadas extras (itens de um `ol reversed`, o `default` de um
///   `epub:switch`, o `rp` vizinho de uma anotação, os filhos de um `svg`
///   candidato a invólucro) olham só os filhos diretos de um elemento, ou um
///   vizinho, uma vez;
/// - âncoras, alvos de link e `id` são mapas; o hash de um `id` (≤ 1 024) ou
///   de um `href` (≤ 4 096) sai uma vez por ocorrência;
/// - os runs se fundem com o anterior em O(1); a folha fecha em O(texto
///   dela) (`text.dart`); cada bloco é congelado uma vez, quando o pai fecha;
/// - todo trabalho paga o orçamento ([irBuildBudget]), e os tetos de texto
///   e de blocos limitam a saída.
library;

import 'package:html/dom.dart';

import '../css/cascade.dart';
import '../css/computed_style.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic.dart';
import '../diagnostics/exceptions.dart';
import 'attributes.dart';
import 'dom_support.dart';
import 'foreign.dart';
import 'model.dart';
import 'text.dart';

/// Texto canônico por seção (§9.12): a folha que passaria é cortada.
const int maxSectionTextLength = 2 * 1024 * 1024;

/// Blocos por seção, folhas e containers (§9.12).
const int maxSectionBlocks = 200000;

/// Passos da caminhada (§9.12, #35): rede de segurança.
const int irBuildBudget = 1 << 24;

/// `id` acima disto é truncado (§9.9).
const int maxIdLength = 1024;

/// A seção sem os diagnósticos (que são do sink, §10).
final class SectionContent {
  SectionContent._({
    required this.lang,
    required this.dir,
    required this.canonicalText,
    required this.blocks,
    required this.links,
    required this.anchors,
  });

  final String? lang;
  final IrDirection dir;
  final String canonicalText;
  final List<Block> blocks;
  final List<LinkTarget> links;
  final Map<String, int> anchors;
}

/// Recebe o resultado de [buildSection], e o que só vira diagnóstico no fim
/// da seção (§10.2, passo 9).
final class BuildResult {
  SectionContent? _content;
  bool _blocksTruncated = false;
  bool _budgetExhausted = false;
  int _duplicateAnchors = 0;
  String? _duplicateSample;

  /// `StateError` antes de o gerador terminar.
  SectionContent get content =>
      _content ?? (throw StateError('buildSection ainda não terminou'));

  /// O teto de blocos cortou a estrutura (motivo `blocks`).
  bool get blocksTruncated => _blocksTruncated;

  /// O orçamento cortou a estrutura (motivo `budget`).
  bool get budgetExhausted => _budgetExhausted;

  /// `id` repetidos descartados (o primeiro vence) e o primeiro deles.
  int get duplicateAnchors => _duplicateAnchors;
  String? get duplicateSample => _duplicateSample;
}

/// Onde a caminhada parou (§9.12): o resto da seção, para
/// [collectRestText].
final class BuildCursor {
  BuildCursor._(this._styles, this._frames);

  final SectionStyles _styles;

  /// (pai, índice do próximo filho), do mais externo ao mais interno.
  final List<(Node, int)> _frames;
}

/// Os `Text` ainda não visitados, em ordem de documento, a partir de [from],
/// sem entrar em `head`, `script`, `style` (de qualquer namespace),
/// `template` nem em escondido; os dados já estão decodificados. Os
/// elementos de bloco viram espaço; colapsa com `white-space: normal`
/// (§8.2); sem NFC e sem `\n`. Para em [maxLength] unidades (#41).
String collectRestText(BuildCursor from, {required int maxLength}) {
  final b = LeafTextBuilder();
  final stack = [...from._frames];

  void feed(String s) {
    for (var k = 0; k < s.length && b.length < maxLength; k += 65536) {
      b.text(
        s.substring(k, k + 65536 < s.length ? k + 65536 : s.length),
        whiteSpace: CssWhiteSpace.normal,
        attrs: 0,
        linkIndex: -1,
      );
    }
  }

  while (stack.isNotEmpty && b.length < maxLength) {
    final (parent, i) = stack.removeLast();
    final nodes = parent.nodes;
    if (i >= nodes.length) continue;
    stack.add((parent, i + 1));
    final child = nodes[i];
    if (child is Text) {
      feed(child.data);
    } else if (child is Element) {
      final style = from._styles.styleOf(child);
      final name = child.localName;
      final html = child.namespaceUri == htmlNamespace;
      if (style == null ||
          style.display == CssDisplay.none ||
          name == 'script' ||
          name == 'style' ||
          html && (name == 'head' || name == 'template' || name == 'title')) {
        continue;
      }
      if (style.display == CssDisplay.block ||
          style.display == CssDisplay.listItem) {
        feed(' ');
      }
      stack.add((child, 0));
    }
  }
  return truncateAtCodePoint(b.rawText, maxLength);
}

/// Caminhada da seção (doc/08 §3): cede a cada folha fechada e a cada
/// [irYieldSourceUnits] unidades de texto; não muda o DOM. [budget] existe
/// para os testes do corte pelo orçamento.
Iterable<void> buildSection(
  Document document,
  SectionStyles styles, {
  required String sectionPath,
  required DiagnosticSink sink,
  required BuildResult into,
  int budget = irBuildBudget,
}) sync* {
  final builder = _Builder(document, styles, sectionPath, sink, budget);
  yield* builder.run();
  into
    .._content = builder.content
    .._blocksTruncated = builder.stop == _StopReason.blocks
    .._budgetExhausted = builder.stop == _StopReason.budget
    .._duplicateAnchors = builder.duplicates
    .._duplicateSample = builder.duplicateSample;
}

enum _StopReason { blocks, budget, text }

/// Lançada para interromper a caminhada antes de consumir o nó corrente.
final class _Stop implements Exception {
  const _Stop();
}

enum _Role { top, flat, container, item, table, group, row }

/// Um elemento aberto na caminhada.
final class _Frame {
  _Frame(this.node, this.element, this.style, this.depth);

  final Node node;
  final Element? element;
  final ComputedStyle style;
  final int depth;

  /// Próximo filho a visitar.
  int index = 0;

  /// Bits de `InlineAttr` do texto direto do elemento.
  int attrs = 0;

  /// Os mesmos, sem link, `noteRef` e anotação: os do `\n` (#18).
  int baseAttrs = 0;
  int linkIndex = -1;
  int sizeStep = 0;
  bool inCode = false;
  bool ruby = false;
  bool verse = false;
  bool titleLike = false;
  String? lang;

  /// O box que o elemento abriu, se é de bloco.
  _Box? box;

  /// `epub:switch`: o índice do `default` (-1 sem ele); -2 se não é.
  int switchDefault = -2;

  /// `rt` de `ruby`/`rtc`: o índice dele no pai (para o `rp` seguinte).
  int rubyIndex = -1;

  /// `rtc`: o texto direto dele é unidade de anotação.
  bool rtc = false;
}

/// Um contexto de bloco: o lugar onde folhas e blocos são emitidos.
final class _Box {
  _Box(this.role, this.frame, this.parent, this.out, {this.implicit = false});

  final _Role role;

  /// De onde vêm `lang`, `dir`, `align` e os atributos do `\n`; nos boxes
  /// implícitos, o do pai.
  final _Frame frame;
  final _Box? parent;

  /// Onde o próprio box (container) entra, ou onde as folhas de um box
  /// achatado entram.
  final List<_Draft> out;
  final bool implicit;

  /// Os filhos de um container, item, tabela ou linha.
  final List<_Draft> children = [];
  _Draft? draft;
  BlockKind leafKind = BlockKind.paragraph;
  int headingLevel = 0;
  int completedAtStart = 0;
  BreakHint breakAfter = BreakHint.auto;

  /// A lista mais próxima (a própria, num `container(list)`).
  _Box? list;
  int nextOrdinal = 1;
  int step = 1;
  int listLevel = 0;

  /// Tabela: a linha implícita aberta; linha: a célula implícita aberta.
  _Box? implicitRow;
  _Box? implicitCell;
  bool headerGroup = false;

  bool get collects =>
      role == _Role.container ||
      role == _Role.item ||
      role == _Role.table ||
      role == _Role.row;

  /// Onde entram as folhas e os blocos de dentro.
  List<_Draft> get sink => collects ? children : out;
}

/// Um bloco em construção (mutável até o pai fechar).
final class _Draft {
  _Draft(this.kind);

  BlockKind kind;
  ContainerKind? container;
  AsideKind? aside;
  int level = 0;
  int textStart = 0;
  int textEnd = 0;
  String? lang;
  IrDirection? dir;
  BlockAlign align = BlockAlign.start;
  bool titleLike = false;
  BreakHint breakBefore = BreakHint.auto;
  BreakHint breakAfter = BreakHint.auto;
  BreakHint breakInside = BreakHint.auto;
  List<StyleRun> runs = const [];
  List<InlineObject> objects = const [];
  List<Block>? children;
  ListMarker? marker;
  int? ordinal;
  int? start;
  bool reversed = false;
  int colSpan = 1;
  int rowSpan = 1;
  bool isHeaderCell = false;
  bool isHeaderRow = false;
  InlineObject? object;
  PlaceholderReason? placeholder;
  String? placeholderDetail;

  /// Folha: o box de que ela é conteúdo próprio; `null` nas folhas que são
  /// blocos à parte (objeto de bloco, `math` de bloco, `hr`).
  _Box? owner;

  Block freeze() => Block(
    kind: kind,
    container: container,
    aside: aside,
    level: level,
    textStart: textStart,
    textEnd: textEnd,
    lang: lang,
    dir: dir,
    align: align,
    titleLike: titleLike,
    breakBefore: breakBefore,
    breakAfter: breakAfter,
    breakInside: breakInside,
    runs: runs,
    objects: objects,
    children: children,
    marker: marker,
    ordinal: ordinal,
    start: start,
    reversed: reversed,
    colSpan: colSpan,
    rowSpan: rowSpan,
    isHeaderCell: isHeaderCell,
    isHeaderRow: isHeaderRow,
    object: object,
    placeholder: placeholder,
    placeholderDetail: placeholderDetail,
  );
}

/// A folha aberta (só há uma por vez: um bloco que começa fecha a corrente).
final class _OpenLeaf {
  _OpenLeaf(this.box, this.kind, this.breakBefore, {this.separate = false});

  final LeafTextBuilder text = LeafTextBuilder();
  final _Box box;
  final BlockKind kind;
  final BreakHint breakBefore;

  /// Bloco à parte (objeto de bloco, `math` de bloco, `hr`): não é conteúdo
  /// próprio do box.
  final bool separate;
  final List<InlineObject> objects = [];
}

BreakHint _breakOf(CssBreak b) => switch (b) {
  CssBreak.auto => BreakHint.auto,
  CssBreak.page => BreakHint.page,
  CssBreak.avoid => BreakHint.avoid,
};

/// O mais forte (CSS Fragmentation 3 §3.1): `page` > `avoid` > `auto`.
BreakHint _strongest(BreakHint a, BreakHint b) =>
    a == BreakHint.page || b == BreakHint.page
    ? BreakHint.page
    : (a == BreakHint.avoid || b == BreakHint.avoid
          ? BreakHint.avoid
          : BreakHint.auto);

IrDirection _dirOf(ComputedStyle s) =>
    s.direction == CssDirection.rtl ? IrDirection.rtl : IrDirection.ltr;

BlockAlign _alignOf(ComputedStyle s) => switch (s.textAlign) {
  CssTextAlign.start => BlockAlign.start,
  CssTextAlign.center => BlockAlign.center,
  CssTextAlign.end => BlockAlign.end,
};

ListMarker _markerOf(CssListStyleType t) => switch (t) {
  CssListStyleType.disc => ListMarker.disc,
  CssListStyleType.circle => ListMarker.circle,
  CssListStyleType.square => ListMarker.square,
  CssListStyleType.decimal => ListMarker.decimal,
  CssListStyleType.lowerAlpha => ListMarker.lowerAlpha,
  CssListStyleType.upperAlpha => ListMarker.upperAlpha,
  CssListStyleType.lowerRoman => ListMarker.lowerRoman,
  CssListStyleType.upperRoman => ListMarker.upperRoman,
  CssListStyleType.none => ListMarker.none,
};

int _saturate(int v) =>
    v > maxOrdinal ? maxOrdinal : (v < minOrdinal ? minOrdinal : v);

bool _isBlockDisplay(ComputedStyle s) =>
    s.display == CssDisplay.block || s.display == CssDisplay.listItem;

/// `head`, `title`, `script`, `style` e `template` saem pelo tipo (§9.1),
/// não só pelo `display: none` da folha padrão.
bool _skippedByType(String? name) =>
    name == 'head' ||
    name == 'title' ||
    name == 'script' ||
    name == 'style' ||
    name == 'template';

const Set<String> _codeElements = {'code', 'kbd', 'samp', 'tt'};
const Set<String> _preElements = {'pre', 'listing', 'xmp', 'plaintext'};
const List<String> _titleTokens = [
  'title',
  'subtitle',
  'fulltitle',
  'halftitle',
  'covertitle',
  'dedication',
  'doc-subtitle',
  'doc-dedication',
];

bool _isSwitch(Element e) =>
    e.namespaceUri == opsNamespace && e.localName == 'switch' ||
    e.namespaceUri == htmlNamespace && e.localName == 'epub:switch';

bool _isDefault(Element e) =>
    e.namespaceUri == opsNamespace && e.localName == 'default' ||
    e.namespaceUri == htmlNamespace && e.localName == 'epub:default';

final class _Builder {
  _Builder(this._document, this._styles, this._path, this._sink, this._budget);

  final Document _document;
  final SectionStyles _styles;
  final String _path;
  final DiagnosticSink _sink;
  final int _budget;

  late SectionContent content;
  _StopReason? stop;
  int duplicates = 0;
  String? duplicateSample;

  final StringBuffer _text = StringBuffer();
  int _length = 0;
  final List<LinkTarget> _links = [];
  final Map<LinkTarget, int> _linkIndex = {};

  /// Âncoras: chaves `#id` e `@nome`, na ordem de registro.
  final List<String> _ids = [];
  final List<String> _names = [];
  final Set<String> _idSet = {};
  final Set<String> _nameSet = {};
  final Map<String, int> _offsets = {};
  final List<String> _pendingAnchors = [];

  final List<_Frame> _frames = [];
  final List<_Box> _boxes = [];
  final List<_Draft> _top = [];
  _OpenLeaf? _leaf;
  BreakHint _pendingBreak = BreakHint.auto;
  _Draft? _lastCompleted;
  int _completed = 0;
  int _blocks = 0;
  int _steps = 0;
  final StepCounter _extra = StepCounter();
  int _units = 0;

  /// Uma folha fechou desde a última cessão.
  bool _leafClosed = false;
  bool _ruby = false;
  bool _math = false;
  String? _sectionLang;
  IrDirection _sectionDir = IrDirection.ltr;

  Iterable<void> run() sync* {
    Element? root;
    for (final n in _document.nodes) {
      if (n is Element) {
        root = n;
        break;
      }
    }
    final rootFrame = _Frame(_document, null, ComputedStyle.initial, 0);
    if (root != null) {
      final lang = cleanLang(rawLangOf(root));
      _sectionLang = lang == null || lang.isEmpty ? null : lang;
      Element? body;
      for (final n in root.nodes) {
        if (n is Element &&
            n.namespaceUri == htmlNamespace &&
            n.localName == 'body') {
          body = n;
          break;
        }
      }
      final dirStyle = _styles.styleOf(body ?? root);
      if (dirStyle != null) _sectionDir = _dirOf(dirStyle);
    }
    rootFrame.lang = _sectionLang;
    _frames.add(rootFrame);
    _boxes.add(_Box(_Role.top, rootFrame, null, _top));

    while (_frames.isNotEmpty) {
      final frame = _frames.last;
      final nodes = frame.node.nodes;
      try {
        if (frame.index >= nodes.length) {
          if (frame.box != null && _leaf != null) yield* _closeLeaf();
          if (stop != null) break;
          _exit(frame);
          continue;
        }
        if (_steps >= _budget) {
          stop = _StopReason.budget;
          break;
        }
        if (_leafClosed || _units >= irYieldSourceUnits) {
          _leafClosed = false;
          _units = 0;
          yield null;
        }
        final child = nodes[frame.index];
        _steps++;
        if (child is Text) {
          if (child.data.length > irYieldSourceUnits) {
            yield* _longText(child, frame);
          } else {
            _textNode(child, frame);
          }
        } else if (child is Element) {
          if (_leaf != null && _opensBlock(child, frame)) yield* _closeLeaf();
          if (stop != null) break;
          _enter(child, frame);
        }
      } on _Stop {
        break;
      }
      if (stop != null) break;
      frame.index++;
    }
    if (_leafClosed) yield null;
    yield* _finish();
  }

  Iterable<void> _finish() sync* {
    final cut = stop;
    // O cursor do resto: no quadro mais interno, o índice aponta o nó que
    // não foi consumido; nos de fora, já aponta o seguinte ao que estava em
    // curso (o índice avança logo depois de entrar no filho).
    final cursor = BuildCursor._(_styles, [
      for (final f in _frames) (f.node, f.index),
    ]);
    if (_leaf != null && cut != _StopReason.text) yield* _closeLeaf();
    _leaf = null;
    // Depois de um corte, os quadros abertos fecham como estão: nenhum texto
    // novo (sem parêntese de ruby, item vazio some).
    while (_frames.isNotEmpty) {
      _exit(_frames.last);
    }
    if (stop == _StopReason.blocks || stop == _StopReason.budget) {
      final room = maxSectionTextLength - _length;
      if (room > 0) {
        final rest = collectRestText(cursor, maxLength: room);
        _steps += rest.length >> 6;
        var first = true;
        for (final leaf in placeholderLeaves(rest, maxTotal: room)) {
          final start = _length;
          final d = _Draft(BlockKind.placeholder)
            ..placeholder = PlaceholderReason.truncated
            ..placeholderDetail = _path
            ..textStart = start
            ..textEnd = start + leaf.length
            ..runs = [StyleRun(start, start + leaf.length, 0)];
          _text.write(leaf);
          _length += leaf.length;
          if (first && leaf.length > 1) {
            for (final key in _pendingAnchors) {
              _offsets[key] = start;
            }
            _pendingAnchors.clear();
          }
          first = false;
          _top.add(d);
        }
      }
    }
    final last = _length > 0 ? _length - 1 : 0;
    for (final key in _pendingAnchors) {
      _offsets[key] = last;
    }
    _pendingAnchors.clear();
    final anchors = <String, int>{};
    for (final id in _ids) {
      anchors[id] = _offsets['#$id'] ?? last;
    }
    for (final name in _names) {
      anchors.putIfAbsent(name, () => _offsets['@$name'] ?? last);
    }
    if (_ruby) {
      _sink.emit(
        EpubDiagnosticCode.rubyFlattened,
        href: _path,
        message: 'ruby achatado: base e anotação entre parênteses',
        onStrict: (m) => EpubSectionParseException(m, href: _path),
      );
    }
    if (_math) {
      _sink.emit(
        EpubDiagnosticCode.unsupportedMath,
        href: _path,
        message: 'MathML renderizado pelo texto (alttext ou elementos-token)',
        onStrict: (m) => EpubSectionParseException(m, href: _path),
      );
    }
    content = SectionContent._(
      lang: _sectionLang,
      dir: _sectionDir,
      canonicalText: _text.toString(),
      blocks: [for (final d in _top) d.freeze()],
      links: _links,
      anchors: anchors,
    );
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- texto

  bool _collapsible(CssWhiteSpace ws) =>
      ws == CssWhiteSpace.normal || ws == CssWhiteSpace.nowrap;

  bool _hiddenBySwitch(_Frame parent) =>
      parent.switchDefault != -2 && parent.index != parent.switchDefault;

  void _textNode(Text node, _Frame frame) {
    final data = node.data;
    _steps += data.length >> 6;
    if (_hiddenBySwitch(frame)) return;
    final ws = frame.style.whiteSpace;
    if (_leaf == null && _collapsible(ws) && isAllAsciiWhitespace(data)) {
      return;
    }
    if (frame.rtc && frame.depth <= maxIrDepth && !isAllAsciiWhitespace(data)) {
      _rubyUnit(frame, frame.index, () {
        _leaf!.text.text(
          data,
          whiteSpace: ws,
          attrs: frame.attrs | InlineAttr.rubyAnnotation,
          linkIndex: frame.linkIndex,
        );
      });
      _units += data.length;
      return;
    }
    _ensureLeaf().text.text(
      data,
      whiteSpace: ws,
      attrs: frame.attrs,
      linkIndex: frame.linkIndex,
    );
    _units += data.length;
  }

  /// Texto acima de 64 Ki: escrito em fatias (cortadas em fronteira de ponto
  /// de código), com uma cessão entre elas (§8.4).
  Iterable<void> _longText(Text node, _Frame frame) sync* {
    final data = node.data;
    _steps += data.length >> 6;
    if (_hiddenBySwitch(frame)) return;
    final ws = frame.style.whiteSpace;
    if (_leaf == null && _collapsible(ws) && isAllAsciiWhitespace(data)) {
      return;
    }
    final leaf = _ensureLeaf();
    var k = 0;
    while (k < data.length) {
      var end = k + irYieldSourceUnits;
      if (end >= data.length) {
        end = data.length;
      } else {
        final c = data.codeUnitAt(end - 1);
        if (c >= 0xD800 && c <= 0xDBFF) end--;
      }
      leaf.text.text(
        data.substring(k, end),
        whiteSpace: ws,
        attrs: frame.attrs | (frame.rtc ? InlineAttr.rubyAnnotation : 0),
        linkIndex: frame.linkIndex,
      );
      k = end;
      yield null;
    }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- blocos

  void _reserveBlock() {
    if (_blocks >= maxSectionBlocks) {
      stop = _StopReason.blocks;
      throw const _Stop();
    }
    _blocks++;
  }

  BreakHint _takeBreak() {
    final b = _pendingBreak;
    _pendingBreak = BreakHint.auto;
    return b;
  }

  /// O box que recebe um bloco ou uma folha nova: dentro de tabela e de
  /// linha, a linha e a célula implícitas (CSS 2.1 §17.2.1).
  _Box _blockParent() {
    var box = _boxes.last;
    if (box.role == _Role.table || box.role == _Role.group) {
      box = _ensureImplicitRow(box);
    }
    if (box.role == _Role.row) box = _ensureImplicitCell(box);
    return box;
  }

  _OpenLeaf _ensureLeaf() {
    final open = _leaf;
    if (open != null) return open;
    final box = _blockParent();
    _reserveBlock();
    var kind = box.leafKind;
    if ((kind == BlockKind.paragraph || kind == BlockKind.code) &&
        box.frame.verse) {
      kind = BlockKind.verse;
    }
    final leaf = _OpenLeaf(box, kind, _takeBreak());
    for (final key in _pendingAnchors) {
      leaf.text.anchor(key);
    }
    _pendingAnchors.clear();
    return _leaf = leaf;
  }

  /// Uma folha que é um bloco à parte (objeto de bloco, `math` de bloco,
  /// `hr`), aberta e fechada já.
  void _separateLeaf(BlockKind kind, void Function(_OpenLeaf leaf) write) {
    final box = _blockParent();
    _reserveBlock();
    final leaf = _OpenLeaf(box, kind, _takeBreak(), separate: true);
    for (final key in _pendingAnchors) {
      leaf.text.anchor(key);
    }
    _pendingAnchors.clear();
    _leaf = leaf;
    write(leaf);
    _closeLeafSync();
  }

  /// Fecha a folha aberta. A NFC de uma folha acima de 64 Ki cede em
  /// fatias (§8.4); a de uma menor roda direto, sem gerador.
  Iterable<void> _closeLeaf() sync* {
    final leaf = _leaf!;
    if (leaf.text.length <= irYieldSourceUnits) {
      _closeLeafSync();
      return;
    }
    _leaf = null;
    final into = LeafTextResult();
    yield* leaf.text.finishSteps(
      baseAttrs: leaf.box.frame.baseAttrs,
      into: into,
    );
    _emitLeaf(leaf, into.leaf);
  }

  void _closeLeafSync() {
    final leaf = _leaf!;
    _leaf = null;
    if (leaf.text.isEmpty && leaf.kind != BlockKind.sceneBreak) {
      _blocks--;
      _pendingBreak = _strongest(_pendingBreak, leaf.breakBefore);
      _pendingAnchors.addAll(leaf.text.pendingAnchors);
      return;
    }
    _emitLeaf(leaf, leaf.text.finish(baseAttrs: leaf.box.frame.baseAttrs));
  }

  /// A folha pronta vai para o texto e para o box dono; o teto de texto
  /// corta a que passaria dele (§9.12).
  void _emitLeaf(_OpenLeaf leaf, LeafText finished) {
    var lt = finished;
    _pendingAnchors.addAll(leaf.text.pendingAnchors);
    final start = _length;
    final room = maxSectionTextLength - start;
    if (lt.text.length > room) {
      stop = _StopReason.text;
      _sink.emit(
        EpubDiagnosticCode.sectionTooLarge,
        href: _path,
        message:
            'texto canônico acima de $maxSectionTextLength unidades; o resto '
            'da seção não é emitido',
        details: {'reason': 'text', 'limit': maxSectionTextLength},
        onStrict: (m) => EpubSectionParseException(m, href: _path),
      );
      if (room < 1) {
        _blocks--;
        return;
      }
      lt = _cut(lt, room);
    }
    final d = _leafDraft(leaf, lt, start);
    _text.write(lt.text);
    _length += lt.text.length;
    for (final (key, offset) in lt.anchors) {
      _offsets[key] = start + offset;
    }
    leaf.box.sink.add(d);
    _completed++;
    _lastCompleted = d;
    _leafClosed = true;
  }

  /// [t] cortada para ter [max] unidades com o `\n` (em fronteira de ponto
  /// de código); as âncoras de depois do corte voltam a pendentes.
  LeafText _cut(LeafText t, int max) {
    var keep = max - 1;
    if (keep > 0) {
      final c = t.text.codeUnitAt(keep - 1);
      if (c >= 0xD800 && c <= 0xDBFF) keep--;
    }
    final runs = <(int, int, int)>[];
    for (final r in t.runs) {
      if (r.$1 <= keep) {
        runs.add(r);
      } else {
        if (runs.isEmpty || runs.last.$1 < keep) runs.add((keep, r.$2, r.$3));
        break;
      }
    }
    final base = t.runs.last;
    if (runs.isNotEmpty && runs.last.$2 == base.$2 && runs.last.$3 == base.$3) {
      runs[runs.length - 1] = (keep + 1, base.$2, base.$3);
    } else {
      runs.add((keep + 1, base.$2, base.$3));
    }
    final anchors = <(String, int)>[];
    for (final a in t.anchors) {
      if (a.$2 < keep) {
        anchors.add(a);
      } else {
        _pendingAnchors.add(a.$1);
      }
    }
    return LeafText('${t.text.substring(0, keep)}\n', runs, [
      for (final o in t.objectOffsets)
        if (o < keep) o,
    ], anchors);
  }

  _Draft _leafDraft(_OpenLeaf leaf, LeafText lt, int start) {
    final box = leaf.box;
    final f = box.frame;
    final d = _Draft(leaf.kind)
      ..textStart = start
      ..textEnd = start + lt.text.length
      ..breakBefore = leaf.breakBefore
      ..owner = leaf.separate ? null : box;
    _blockProps(d, f);
    if (box.role == _Role.flat || box.role == _Role.item) {
      d.breakInside = _breakOf(f.style.breakInside);
    } else {
      d.breakInside = BreakHint.auto;
    }
    if (leaf.kind == BlockKind.heading) d.level = box.headingLevel;
    final runs = <StyleRun>[];
    var runStart = start;
    for (final (end, attrs, link) in lt.runs) {
      runs.add(StyleRun(runStart, start + end, attrs, link));
      runStart = start + end;
    }
    d.runs = runs;
    final objects = <InlineObject>[
      for (var i = 0; i < lt.objectOffsets.length; i++)
        leaf.objects[i].at(start + lt.objectOffsets[i]),
    ];
    d.objects = objects;
    if (objects.length == 1 && lt.text == '\u{FFFC}\n') {
      d
        ..kind = BlockKind.object
        ..object = objects.single;
    }
    return d;
  }

  /// `lang`, `dir`, `align` e `titleLike` do dono (§9.10, §9.1).
  void _blockProps(_Draft d, _Frame f) {
    d
      ..lang = f.lang != _sectionLang ? f.lang : null
      ..dir = _dirOf(f.style) != _sectionDir ? _dirOf(f.style) : null
      ..align = _alignOf(f.style)
      ..titleLike = f.titleLike;
  }

  _Box _ensureImplicitRow(_Box table) {
    final open = table.implicitRow;
    if (open != null) return open;
    _reserveBlock();
    final row = _Box(
      _Role.row,
      table.frame,
      table,
      table.role == _Role.table ? table.children : table.out,
      implicit: true,
    );
    row.draft = _Draft(BlockKind.container)
      ..container = ContainerKind.tableRow
      ..isHeaderRow = table.headerGroup
      ..breakBefore = _takeBreak();
    _blockProps(row.draft!, table.frame);
    row.completedAtStart = _completed;
    _boxes.add(row);
    return table.implicitRow = row;
  }

  _Box _ensureImplicitCell(_Box row) {
    final open = row.implicitCell;
    if (open != null) return open;
    _reserveBlock();
    final cell = _Box(_Role.item, row.frame, row, row.children, implicit: true);
    cell.draft = _Draft(BlockKind.tableCell)..breakBefore = _takeBreak();
    _blockProps(cell.draft!, row.frame);
    cell.completedAtStart = _completed;
    _boxes.add(cell);
    return row.implicitCell = cell;
  }

  /// Fecha os boxes acima de [box] (os implícitos) e o próprio [box].
  void _closeDownTo(_Box box) {
    if (_leaf != null) _closeLeafSync();
    while (!identical(_boxes.last, box)) {
      _finishBox(_boxes.removeLast());
    }
    _finishBox(_boxes.removeLast());
  }

  void _closeImplicitCell(_Box row) {
    final cell = row.implicitCell;
    if (cell != null) _closeDownTo(cell);
  }

  void _closeImplicitRow(_Box table) {
    final row = table.implicitRow;
    if (row != null) _closeDownTo(row);
  }

  void _finishBox(_Box box) {
    if (box.implicit) {
      if (box.role == _Role.row) box.parent!.implicitRow = null;
      if (box.role == _Role.item) box.parent!.implicitCell = null;
    }
    final draft = box.draft;
    if (draft == null) return; // achatado, grupo, topo
    final children = box.children;
    if (box.role == _Role.item) {
      if (children.isEmpty) {
        // listItem e tableCell vazios: "\n" (#20).
        if (stop != null || _length >= maxSectionTextLength) {
          _blocks--;
          return;
        }
        draft
          ..textStart = _length
          ..textEnd = _length + 1
          ..runs = [StyleRun(_length, _length + 1, box.frame.baseAttrs)];
        _text.write('\n');
        _length++;
        _complete(box, draft);
        return;
      }
      final only = children.length == 1 ? children.single : null;
      if (only != null && identical(only.owner, box) && only.children == null) {
        // Só conteúdo inline: a folha é o próprio item (§9.2).
        only
          ..kind = draft.kind
          ..object = null
          ..marker = draft.marker
          ..ordinal = draft.ordinal
          ..level = draft.level
          ..colSpan = draft.colSpan
          ..rowSpan = draft.rowSpan
          ..isHeaderCell = draft.isHeaderCell
          ..breakBefore = _strongest(draft.breakBefore, only.breakBefore);
        _blocks--;
        _complete(box, only);
        return;
      }
    } else if (children.isEmpty) {
      // Container sem filhos some; o break-before dele volta a pendente.
      _blocks--;
      _pendingBreak = _strongest(_pendingBreak, draft.breakBefore);
      return;
    } else if (draft.container == ContainerKind.generic &&
        children.length == 1) {
      final only = children.single
        ..breakInside = _strongest(children.single.breakInside, BreakHint.avoid)
        ..breakBefore = _strongest(
          draft.breakBefore,
          children.single.breakBefore,
        );
      _blocks--;
      _complete(box, only);
      return;
    }
    draft
      ..children = [for (final c in children) c.freeze()]
      ..textStart = children.first.textStart
      ..textEnd = children.last.textEnd;
    _complete(box, draft);
  }

  void _complete(_Box box, _Draft d) {
    box.out.add(d);
    _completed++;
    _lastCompleted = d;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- âncoras

  void _anchor(String key) {
    final leaf = _leaf;
    if (leaf != null) {
      leaf.text.anchor(key);
    } else {
      _pendingAnchors.add(key);
    }
  }

  /// `id` (qualquer namespace) e `a[name]` (§9.9, #30).
  void _registerId(Element e) {
    final raw = e.attributes['id'];
    if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
      final id = truncateAtCodePoint(raw, maxIdLength);
      if (_idSet.add(id)) {
        _ids.add(id);
        _anchor('#$id');
      } else {
        duplicates++;
        duplicateSample ??= truncateAtCodePoint(id, 64);
      }
    }
    if (e.localName == 'a' && e.namespaceUri == htmlNamespace) {
      final raw = e.attributes['name'];
      if (raw != null && raw.isNotEmpty) {
        final name = truncateAtCodePoint(raw, maxIdLength);
        if (_nameSet.add(name)) {
          _names.add(name);
          _anchor('@$name');
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /// Os `id` de uma subárvore escondida (ou de `svg`/`math`), que viram
  /// âncora do próximo caractere emitido; 1 passo por nó.
  void _subtreeIds(Element root) {
    final stack = <Node>[root];
    while (stack.isNotEmpty) {
      final n = stack.removeLast();
      _steps++;
      if (n is! Element) continue;
      if (!identical(n, root) && _styles.styleOf(n) == null) continue;
      _registerId(n);
      final c = n.nodes;
      for (var i = c.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        stack.add(c[i]);
      }
    }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- elementos

  /// Entrar em [e] fecha a folha aberta (é de bloco)?
  bool _opensBlock(Element e, _Frame parent) {
    final style = _styles.styleOf(e);
    if (style == null || style.display == CssDisplay.none) return false;
    if (e.namespaceUri == htmlNamespace && _skippedByType(e.localName)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (_hiddenBySwitch(parent) || parent.depth + 1 > maxIrDepth) return false;
    return _isBlockDisplay(style);
  }

  void _enter(Element e, _Frame parent) {
    final style = _styles.styleOf(e);
    _steps += e.attributes.length;
    if (style == null) return; // subárvore de template
    final ns = e.namespaceUri;
    final name = e.localName;
    final html = ns == htmlNamespace;
    if (html && _skippedByType(name)) return;
    if (_hiddenBySwitch(parent) || style.display == CssDisplay.none) {
      _subtreeIds(e);
      return;
    }
    final depth = parent.depth + 1;
    final f = _Frame(e, e, style, depth);
    if (depth > maxIrDepth) {
      // Transparente: só o texto e as âncoras (§9.1).
      f
        ..attrs = parent.attrs
        ..baseAttrs = parent.baseAttrs
        ..linkIndex = parent.linkIndex
        ..lang = parent.lang
        ..verse = parent.verse
        ..sizeStep = parent.sizeStep;
      _registerId(e);
      _frames.add(f);
      return;
    }
    final block = _isBlockDisplay(style);
    if (block && _leaf != null) _closeLeafSync();
    _inherit(f, parent, e, style, html, name);

    if (html) {
      switch (name) {
        case 'br':
          _registerId(e);
          _ensureLeaf().text.lineBreak(attrs: f.attrs, linkIndex: f.linkIndex);
          return;
        case 'wbr':
          _registerId(e);
          _ensureLeaf().text.wordBreak(attrs: f.attrs, linkIndex: f.linkIndex);
          return;
        case 'img':
          _pendBreakBefore(style, block);
          _registerId(e);
          _object(
            InlineObject(
              offset: 0,
              kind: ObjectKind.image,
              href: resolveImageSource(e.attributes['src'], sectionPath: _path),
              intrinsicWidth: parseHtmlDimension(e.attributes['width']),
              intrinsicHeight: parseHtmlDimension(e.attributes['height']),
              alt: _attrText(e, 'alt'),
              title: _attrText(e, 'title'),
            ),
            f,
            block,
          );
          return;
        case 'hr' when block:
          _pendingBreak = _strongest(
            _pendingBreak,
            _breakOf(style.breakBefore),
          );
          _registerId(e);
          final before = _completed;
          _separateLeaf(BlockKind.sceneBreak, (_) {});
          _breakAfter(style, before);
          return;
        case 'col' || 'colgroup':
          return;
      }
    }
    if (ns == svgNamespace && name == 'svg') {
      _pendBreakBefore(style, block);
      _subtreeIds(e);
      _object(_svgObject(e), f, block);
      return;
    }
    if (ns == mathmlNamespace && name == 'math') {
      _mathElement(e, f, style);
      return;
    }
    if (_isSwitch(e)) {
      f.switchDefault = -1;
      final nodes = e.nodes;
      for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
        _steps++;
        final n = nodes[i];
        if (n is Element && _isDefault(n)) {
          f.switchDefault = i;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (html) {
      final parentElement = parent.element;
      final inRuby =
          parentElement != null &&
          parentElement.namespaceUri == htmlNamespace &&
          (parentElement.localName == 'ruby' ||
              parentElement.localName == 'rtc');
      if (name == 'ruby') _ruby = true;
      if (name == 'rtc') f.rtc = true;
      if (name == 'rt' && inRuby) {
        f.rubyIndex = parent.index;
        if (!_visibleRp(parent, parent.index, -1)) {
          _ensureLeaf().text.text(
            '(',
            whiteSpace: CssWhiteSpace.normal,
            attrs: parent.attrs & ~InlineAttr.rubyAnnotation,
            linkIndex: parent.linkIndex,
          );
        }
        f
          ..ruby = true
          ..attrs |= InlineAttr.rubyAnnotation;
      }
    }
    if (block) {
      _pendingBreak = _strongest(_pendingBreak, _breakOf(style.breakBefore));
      f.box = _openBox(e, f, style, html, name);
    }
    _registerId(e);
    _frames.add(f);
  }

  void _pendBreakBefore(ComputedStyle style, bool block) {
    if (block) {
      _pendingBreak = _strongest(_pendingBreak, _breakOf(style.breakBefore));
    }
  }

  /// `break-after` de um elemento de bloco (#45): no último bloco emitido
  /// dentro dele; sem nenhum, no próximo.
  void _breakAfter(ComputedStyle style, int completedBefore) {
    final after = _breakOf(style.breakAfter);
    if (after == BreakHint.auto) return;
    final last = _lastCompleted;
    if (_completed > completedBefore && last != null) {
      last.breakAfter = _strongest(last.breakAfter, after);
    } else {
      _pendingBreak = _strongest(_pendingBreak, after);
    }
  }

  String? _attrText(Element e, String name) {
    final v = e.attributes[name];
    return v == null ? null : collapseAsciiWhitespace(v);
  }

  /// Atributos herdados e os do próprio elemento (§9.11).
  void _inherit(
    _Frame f,
    _Frame parent,
    Element e,
    ComputedStyle style,
    bool html,
    String? name,
  ) {
    var step =
        parent.sizeStep +
        switch (style.fontSizeStep) {
          CssFontSizeStep.larger => 1,
          CssFontSizeStep.smaller => -1,
          CssFontSizeStep.same => 0,
        };
    if (step > 4) step = 4;
    if (step < -4) step = -4;
    f
      ..sizeStep = step
      ..inCode = parent.inCode || html && _codeElements.contains(name)
      ..lang = cleanLang(rawLangOf(e)) ?? parent.lang;
    final type = e.attributes['epub:type'];
    final role = e.attributes['role'];
    f.verse =
        parent.verse ||
        hasToken(type, 'z3998:poem') ||
        hasToken(type, 'z3998:verse') ||
        hasToken(role, 'z3998:poem') ||
        hasToken(role, 'z3998:verse');
    for (final t in _titleTokens) {
      if (hasToken(type, t) || hasToken(role, t)) {
        f.titleLike = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    var bits = 0;
    if (style.fontStyle == CssFontStyle.italic) bits |= InlineAttr.emphasis;
    if (style.fontWeight == CssFontWeight.bold) bits |= InlineAttr.strong;
    if (f.inCode) bits |= InlineAttr.code;
    if (style.verticalAlign == CssVerticalAlign.sup) {
      bits |= InlineAttr.superscript;
    } else if (style.verticalAlign == CssVerticalAlign.sub) {
      bits |= InlineAttr.subscript;
    }
    if (style.fontVariant == CssFontVariant.smallCaps) {
      bits |= InlineAttr.smallCaps;
    }
    if (style.lineThrough) bits |= InlineAttr.strikethrough;
    if (style.underline) bits |= InlineAttr.underline;
    switch (style.textTransform) {
      case CssTextTransform.uppercase:
        bits |= InlineAttr.uppercase;
      case CssTextTransform.lowercase:
        bits |= InlineAttr.lowercase;
      case CssTextTransform.capitalize:
        bits |= InlineAttr.capitalize;
      case CssTextTransform.none:
        break;
    }
    if (step < 0) bits |= InlineAttr.sizeSmaller;
    if (step > 0) bits |= InlineAttr.sizeLarger;
    if (style.whiteSpace == CssWhiteSpace.pre ||
        style.whiteSpace == CssWhiteSpace.preWrap) {
      bits |= InlineAttr.preserveSpace;
    }
    f.baseAttrs = bits;
    // Link: o `a` mais interno com href vale (§9.5).
    var link = parent.linkIndex;
    var linkBits = parent.attrs & (InlineAttr.link | InlineAttr.noteRef);
    if (html && name == 'a') {
      final href = e.attributes['href'];
      if (href != null) {
        final target = resolveLink(href, sectionPath: _path);
        if (target == null) {
          link = -1;
          linkBits = 0;
        } else {
          link = _linkIndex.putIfAbsent(target, () {
            _links.add(target);
            return _links.length - 1;
          });
          final note =
              !target.isExternal &&
              (hasToken(type, 'noteref') || hasToken(role, 'doc-noteref'));
          linkBits = note ? InlineAttr.noteRef : InlineAttr.link;
        }
      }
    }
    f
      ..linkIndex = link
      ..ruby = parent.ruby
      ..attrs = bits | linkBits | (parent.ruby ? InlineAttr.rubyAnnotation : 0);
  }

  /// Um objeto: inline na folha corrente, ou um bloco `object` (§9.6).
  void _object(InlineObject object, _Frame f, bool block) {
    final before = _completed;
    if (block) {
      _separateLeaf(BlockKind.paragraph, (leaf) {
        leaf.objects.add(object);
        leaf.text.object(attrs: f.attrs, linkIndex: f.linkIndex);
      });
      _breakAfter(f.style, before);
      return;
    }
    final leaf = _ensureLeaf()..objects.add(object);
    leaf.text.object(attrs: f.attrs, linkIndex: f.linkIndex);
  }

  /// SVG (§9.7): o invólucro vira imagem; o resto, objeto `svg` com o markup.
  InlineObject _svgObject(Element svg) {
    final image = wrappedImage(svg, _extra);
    final InlineObject object;
    if (image != null) {
      final (w, h) = svgSize(svg, image);
      object = InlineObject(
        offset: 0,
        kind: ObjectKind.image,
        href: resolveImageSource(imageHref(image), sectionPath: _path),
        intrinsicWidth: w,
        intrinsicHeight: h,
        alt: svgAlt(svg, _extra, useText: false),
      );
    } else {
      final (w, h) = svgSize(svg);
      object = InlineObject(
        offset: 0,
        kind: ObjectKind.svg,
        href: '',
        intrinsicWidth: w,
        intrinsicHeight: h,
        alt: svgAlt(svg, _extra, useText: true),
        svgMarkup: serializeSvg(svg, _extra),
      );
    }
    _steps += _extra.steps;
    _extra.steps = 0;
    return object;
  }

  /// MathML (§9.8): o texto da fórmula; `display="block"` faz folha própria.
  void _mathElement(Element e, _Frame f, ComputedStyle style) {
    _math = true;
    final display = e.attributes['display'];
    final block =
        _isBlockDisplay(style) ||
        display != null && asciiLower(trimAsciiWhitespace(display)) == 'block';
    if (block) {
      if (_leaf != null) _closeLeafSync();
      _pendingBreak = _strongest(_pendingBreak, _breakOf(style.breakBefore));
    }
    _subtreeIds(e);
    final text = mathText(e, _extra);
    _steps += _extra.steps;
    _extra.steps = 0;
    if (text.isEmpty) return;
    final before = _completed;
    if (block) {
      _separateLeaf(BlockKind.paragraph, (leaf) {
        leaf.text.text(
          text,
          whiteSpace: CssWhiteSpace.normal,
          attrs: f.attrs,
          linkIndex: f.linkIndex,
        );
      });
      _breakAfter(style, before);
      return;
    }
    _ensureLeaf().text.text(
      text,
      whiteSpace: CssWhiteSpace.normal,
      attrs: f.attrs,
      linkIndex: f.linkIndex,
    );
  }

  /// O vizinho de uma anotação, na [direction] (-1 antes, +1 depois),
  /// ignorando um texto só de espaço, é um `rp` visível (#47)?
  bool _visibleRp(_Frame parent, int index, int direction) {
    final nodes = parent.node.nodes;
    var k = index + direction;
    if (k >= 0 && k < nodes.length) {
      final n = nodes[k];
      if (n is Text && isAllAsciiWhitespace(n.data)) k += direction;
    }
    if (k < 0 || k >= nodes.length) return false;
    final n = nodes[k];
    if (n is! Element ||
        n.namespaceUri != htmlNamespace ||
        n.localName != 'rp') {
      return false;
    }
    final s = _styles.styleOf(n);
    return s != null && s.display != CssDisplay.none;
  }

  /// Texto direto de `rtc` (§9.8): uma unidade com os seus parênteses.
  void _rubyUnit(_Frame rtc, int index, void Function() write) {
    final paren = rtc.attrs & ~InlineAttr.rubyAnnotation;
    final leaf = _ensureLeaf();
    if (!_visibleRp(rtc, index, -1)) {
      leaf.text.text(
        '(',
        whiteSpace: CssWhiteSpace.normal,
        attrs: paren,
        linkIndex: rtc.linkIndex,
      );
    }
    write();
    if (!_visibleRp(rtc, index, 1)) {
      leaf.text.text(
        ')',
        whiteSpace: CssWhiteSpace.normal,
        attrs: paren,
        linkIndex: rtc.linkIndex,
      );
    }
  }

  void _exit(_Frame frame) {
    _frames.removeLast();
    if (frame.rubyIndex >= 0 && stop == null) {
      final parent = _frames.last;
      if (!_visibleRp(parent, frame.rubyIndex, 1)) {
        _ensureLeaf().text.text(
          ')',
          whiteSpace: CssWhiteSpace.normal,
          attrs: parent.attrs & ~InlineAttr.rubyAnnotation,
          linkIndex: parent.linkIndex,
        );
      }
    }
    final box = frame.box;
    if (box == null) return;
    final before = box.completedAtStart;
    _closeDownTo(box);
    _breakAfter(frame.style, before);
  }

  /// O box de um elemento de bloco, na ordem de §9.1.
  _Box _openBox(
    Element e,
    _Frame f,
    ComputedStyle style,
    bool html,
    String? name,
  ) {
    // O box estrutural do pai no DOM (sem os implícitos), para os papéis de
    // tabela, que só valem com o ancestral certo (#44).
    var structural = _boxes.last;
    while (structural.implicit) {
      structural = structural.parent!;
    }
    final roleParent = identical(structural.frame.element, e.parentNode)
        ? structural
        : null;

    if (html && name == 'table') {
      return _container(ContainerKind.table, _Role.table, f, style);
    }
    if (html && roleParent != null) {
      final role = roleParent.role;
      if (role == _Role.table && name == 'caption') {
        _closeImplicitRow(roleParent);
        return _flat(f, roleParent.children, BlockKind.caption);
      }
      if (role == _Role.table &&
          (name == 'thead' || name == 'tbody' || name == 'tfoot')) {
        _closeImplicitRow(roleParent);
        final group = _Box(_Role.group, f, roleParent, roleParent.children)
          ..headerGroup = name == 'thead'
          ..list = roleParent.list
          ..completedAtStart = _completed;
        _boxes.add(group);
        return group;
      }
      if ((role == _Role.table || role == _Role.group) && name == 'tr') {
        _closeImplicitRow(roleParent);
        final row = _container(
          ContainerKind.tableRow,
          _Role.row,
          f,
          style,
          out: role == _Role.table ? roleParent.children : roleParent.out,
        );
        row.draft!.isHeaderRow = roleParent.headerGroup;
        return row;
      }
      if (name == 'td' || name == 'th') {
        _Box? row;
        if (role == _Role.table || role == _Role.group) {
          row = _ensureImplicitRow(roleParent);
        } else if (role == _Role.row) {
          row = roleParent;
        }
        if (row != null) {
          _closeImplicitCell(row);
          _reserveBlock();
          final cell = _Box(_Role.item, f, row, row.children)
            ..list = row.list
            ..completedAtStart = _completed;
          cell.draft = _Draft(BlockKind.tableCell)
            ..colSpan = spanOf(e.attributes['colspan'])
            ..rowSpan = spanOf(e.attributes['rowspan'])
            ..isHeaderCell = name == 'th'
            ..breakBefore = _takeBreak()
            ..breakInside = _breakOf(style.breakInside);
          _blockProps(cell.draft!, f);
          _boxes.add(cell);
          return cell;
        }
      }
    }
    if (html &&
        (name == 'ol' || name == 'ul' || name == 'menu' || name == 'dir')) {
      final list = _container(ContainerKind.list, _Role.container, f, style);
      final parentLevel = _boxes[_boxes.length - 2].list?.listLevel ?? 0;
      list
        ..list = list
        ..listLevel = parentLevel >= 255 ? 255 : parentLevel + 1;
      list.draft!.level = list.listLevel;
      if (name == 'ol') {
        final reversed = e.attributes.containsKey('reversed');
        final startAttr = e.attributes['start'];
        var start = startAttr == null ? null : parseHtmlInteger(startAttr);
        if (start == null && reversed) {
          var count = 0;
          for (final n in e.nodes) {
            _steps++;
            if (n is Element &&
                _styles.styleOf(n)?.display == CssDisplay.listItem) {
              count++;
            }
          }
          start = count;
        }
        start ??= 1;
        list
          ..nextOrdinal = start
          ..step = reversed ? -1 : 1;
        list.draft!
          ..start = start
          ..reversed = reversed;
      }
      return list;
    }
    if (style.display == CssDisplay.listItem) {
      final parentBox = _blockParent();
      _reserveBlock();
      final listBox = parentBox.list;
      final valueAttr = e.attributes['value'];
      final value = valueAttr == null ? null : parseHtmlInteger(valueAttr);
      final ordinal = value ?? listBox?.nextOrdinal ?? 1;
      if (listBox != null) {
        listBox.nextOrdinal = _saturate(ordinal + listBox.step);
      }
      final item = _Box(_Role.item, f, parentBox, parentBox.sink)
        ..list = listBox
        ..completedAtStart = _completed;
      item.draft = _Draft(BlockKind.listItem)
        ..marker = _markerOf(style.listStyleType)
        ..ordinal = ordinal
        ..level = listBox?.listLevel ?? 1
        ..breakBefore = _takeBreak()
        ..breakInside = _breakOf(style.breakInside);
      _blockProps(item.draft!, f);
      _boxes.add(item);
      return item;
    }
    if (html && name == 'blockquote') {
      return _container(ContainerKind.blockquote, _Role.container, f, style);
    }
    if (html && name == 'figure') {
      return _container(ContainerKind.figure, _Role.container, f, style);
    }
    final type = e.attributes['epub:type'];
    final role = e.attributes['role'];
    final AsideKind? noteKind = hasToken(type, 'footnote')
        ? AsideKind.footnote
        : hasToken(type, 'endnote')
        ? AsideKind.endnote
        : hasToken(type, 'rearnote')
        ? AsideKind.rearnote
        : hasToken(role, 'doc-footnote')
        ? AsideKind.footnote
        : hasToken(role, 'doc-endnote')
        ? AsideKind.endnote
        : null;
    if (html && name == 'aside' || noteKind != null) {
      final box = _container(ContainerKind.aside, _Role.container, f, style);
      box.draft!.aside = noteKind ?? AsideKind.aside;
      return box;
    }
    if (html &&
        name != null &&
        name.length == 2 &&
        name.codeUnitAt(0) == 0x68) {
      final level = name.codeUnitAt(1) - 0x30;
      if (level >= 1 && level <= 6) {
        return _flat(f, _blockParent().sink, BlockKind.heading)
          ..headingLevel = level;
      }
    }
    if (html && (name == 'figcaption' || name == 'caption')) {
      return _flat(f, _blockParent().sink, BlockKind.caption);
    }
    if (html && (_preElements.contains(name) || _codeElements.contains(name))) {
      return _flat(f, _blockParent().sink, BlockKind.code);
    }
    if (style.breakInside == CssBreak.avoid) {
      return _container(ContainerKind.generic, _Role.container, f, style);
    }
    return _flat(f, _blockParent().sink, BlockKind.paragraph);
  }

  _Box _flat(_Frame f, List<_Draft> out, BlockKind leafKind) {
    final parent = _boxes.last;
    final box = _Box(_Role.flat, f, parent, out)
      ..leafKind = leafKind
      ..list = parent.list
      ..completedAtStart = _completed;
    _boxes.add(box);
    return box;
  }

  _Box _container(
    ContainerKind kind,
    _Role role,
    _Frame f,
    ComputedStyle style, {
    List<_Draft>? out,
  }) {
    final parent = out == null ? _blockParent() : _boxes.last;
    _reserveBlock();
    final box = _Box(role, f, parent, out ?? parent.sink)
      ..list = parent.list
      ..completedAtStart = _completed;
    box.draft = _Draft(BlockKind.container)
      ..container = kind
      ..breakBefore = _takeBreak()
      ..breakInside = _breakOf(style.breakInside);
    _blockProps(box.draft!, f);
    _boxes.add(box);
    return box;
  }
}
```

---

### Tarefa 12: Orquestrador em três etapas

Spec §10 e #34, #36, #48, #50, #52. `fetchSection` (etapa 1) devolve a seção
pronta quando não há o que parsear — placeholder `"\n"` de item ausente,
remoto ou sem suporte, com o diagnóstico de novo no sink da seção (#36);
bloco `object` de imagem; placeholder `tooLarge` com o texto cru dos
primeiros 8 MiB acima de 16 MiB (#34); placeholder `unreadable` com
`resourceUnreadable` quando o contêiner não lê — ou os bytes, que entram na
chave do cache. `prepareSection` (etapa 2) decodifica, parseia e carrega as
folhas: depois dela o sub-projeto 5 tem a chave. `buildPrepared` (etapa 3,
fatiável) roda a cascata e a construção e emite os agregados do fim
(`unknownEntity`, `sectionTruncated` com os motivos juntos, `anchorDuplicate`).
A exceção de `strict` dos dois sinks relança por identidade; a do
`onCheckpoint` propaga sem virar placeholder.

**Por que é linear:** cada etapa chama as funções lineares das tarefas
anteriores uma vez; o placeholder de seção grande lê só o prefixo de 8 MiB
(`decodeSectionPrefix`, `extractRawText` até 2 Mi, `placeholderLeaves`); o
`onCheckpoint` é chamado uma vez por passo do `decode()` e por `yield` do
parse e da etapa 3. O teste mede 16 MiB + 1.

**Decisões do plano que valem aqui** (texto da seção "Decisões onde a
spec é ambígua"; onde divergem da letra da spec, valem estas):

- **13.** **Resultados com o estado do fim da seção.** `BuildResult` expõe
  `blocksTruncated`, `budgetExhausted`, `duplicateAnchors` e
  `duplicateSample`; `ParsedXhtml` e `TolerantResult`, `entities` e
  `truncatedAttributes`. Os três agregados (`unknownEntity`,
  `sectionTruncated`, `anchorDuplicate`) saem no fim da etapa 3, nessa ordem
  (§10.2, passo 9); o builder emite só `sectionTooLarge` (texto),
  `rubyFlattened` e `unsupportedMath`.
- **17.** **Orquestrador.** O teto de 16 MiB vale sobre `bytes.length`; um provider não
  declara tamanho (`PendingResource.ready` usa `bytes.length`), então o caso
  "provider que declara `size` menor" de §15 não é construível, e o teste cobre
  16 MiB e 16 MiB + 1. O `decode()` é iterado à mão, para o `onCheckpoint`
  ficar fora das capturas: a exceção dele propaga mesmo sendo `EpubException`.

**Arquivos:**
- Criar: `lib/src/ir/section_reader.dart`
- Teste: `test/ir/section_reader_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consome: `EpubContainer.fetch`, `PendingResource.decode()`/`bytes` (`lib/src/container/container.dart`); `SpineItem`, `ManifestItem`, `SectionKind` (`lib/src/publication/model.dart`); `decodeSection`, `decodeSectionPrefix` (Tarefa 4); `EntityRecorder` (Tarefa 5); `maxAttributesPerElement` (Tarefa 6); `parseXhtml`, `ParseResult` (Tarefa 7); `extractRawText`, `placeholderLeaves`, `truncateAtCodePoint` (Tarefa 8); `buildSection`, `BuildResult`, `maxSectionTextLength`, `maxSectionBlocks`, `irBuildBudget` (Tarefa 11); `loadSectionSheets`, `StyleSheetCache`, `SectionSheets`; `computeStyles`, `CascadeResult`.
- Produz: `const int maxSectionBytes = 16 * 1024 * 1024;`, `const int maxPlaceholderSourceBytes = 8 * 1024 * 1024;`.
- Produz: `sealed class SectionSource`, `final class ReadySection extends SectionSource { Section section; }`, `final class SectionBytes extends SectionSource { SpineItem item; Uint8List bytes; }`, `final class PreparedSection { SectionBytes source; Document document; SectionSheets sheets; bool viaXml; EntityRecorder entities; bool truncatedAttributes; }`, `final class SectionResult { Section get section; }`.
- Produz: `Future<SectionSource> fetchSection(EpubContainer container, SpineItem item, {required DiagnosticSink sink, required DiagnosticSink containerSink, FutureOr<void> Function()? onCheckpoint})`, `Future<PreparedSection> prepareSection(SectionBytes source, EpubContainer container, {required StyleSheetCache sheetCache, required DiagnosticSink sink, required DiagnosticSink containerSink, FutureOr<void> Function()? onCheckpoint})`, `Iterable<void> buildPrepared(PreparedSection prepared, {required DiagnosticSink sink, required SectionResult into})`, `Future<Section> readSection(EpubContainer container, SpineItem item, {required StyleSheetCache sheetCache, required DiagnosticSink sink, required DiagnosticSink containerSink, FutureOr<void> Function()? onCheckpoint})`.

Criar `test/ir/section_reader_test.dart`:

```dart
// O orquestrador (spec do IR §10): cada linha de §10.1, o caminho XHTML em
// três etapas (#48), o teto de bytes (#34), as falhas do contêiner e o
// strict pelos dois sinks (§10.3), o onCheckpoint e os agregados do fim
// (#50, #52).
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/container/byte_source.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/container/container.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/container/provider_container.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/container/zip/zip_container.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/css/loader.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/diagnostics/diagnostic.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/diagnostics/exceptions.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/builder.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/model.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/ir/section_reader.dart';
import 'package:galley/src/publication/model.dart';

import '../container/support/zip_fixtures.dart';
import 'support/ir_fixtures.dart';

const _path = 'OEBPS/Text/c.xhtml';

SpineItem _item(
  String path, {
  String mediaType = 'application/xhtml+xml',
  bool missing = false,
  bool remote = false,
}) {
  final m = ManifestItem(
    id: 'c',
    path: path,
    mediaType: mediaType,
    missing: missing,
    remote: remote,
  );
  return SpineItem(idref: 'c', item: m, content: m, linear: true);
}

Future<ProviderContainer> _provider(
  Map<String, Object?> files, {
  Set<String> failingRead = const {},
}) => ProviderContainer.open(
  MapProvider(files, failingRead: failingRead),
  sink: DiagnosticSink(),
);

Future<Section> _read(
  EpubContainer container,
  SpineItem item, {
  DiagnosticSink? sink,
  DiagnosticSink? containerSink,
  Future<void> Function()? onCheckpoint,
}) => readSection(
  container,
  item,
  sheetCache: StyleSheetCache(),
  sink: sink ?? DiagnosticSink(),
  containerSink: containerSink ?? DiagnosticSink(),
  onCheckpoint: onCheckpoint,
);

List<String> _codes(Section s) => [for (final d in s.diagnostics) d.code.name];

void main() {
  group('§10.1 por tipo de item', () {
    test(
      'missing, remote e unsupported: placeholder "\\n" com o diagnóstico',
      () async {
        final c = await _provider({});
        final missing = await _read(c, _item(_path, missing: true));
        final remote = await _read(
          c,
          _item('http://x.org/c.xhtml', remote: true),
        );
        final unsupported = await _read(
          c,
          _item('OEBPS/a.pdf', mediaType: 'application/pdf'),
        );
        for (final (s, reason, detail) in [
          (missing, PlaceholderReason.missing, _path),
          (remote, PlaceholderReason.remote, 'http://x.org/c.xhtml'),
          (
            unsupported,
            PlaceholderReason.unsupportedMediaType,
            'application/pdf',
          ),
        ]) {
          expect(s.canonicalText, '\n');
          final b = s.blocks.single;
          expect(
            (b.kind, b.placeholder, b.placeholderDetail),
            (BlockKind.placeholder, reason, detail),
          );
          expect(checkSection(s), isNull);
        }
        expect(missing.diagnostics.single.details, {
          'reason': 'missing',
          'count': 1,
        });
        expect(remote.diagnostics.single.details['reason'], 'remote');
        expect(remote.href, 'http://x.org/c.xhtml');
        expect(_codes(unsupported), ['unsupportedMediaType']);
        expect(
          unsupported.diagnostics.single.details['mediaType'],
          'application/pdf',
        );
      },
    );

    test('imagem: um bloco object com o caminho, sem tamanho', () async {
      final c = await _provider({
        'OEBPS/i.png': [1, 2, 3],
      });
      final s = await _read(c, _item('OEBPS/i.png', mediaType: 'image/png'));
      expect(s.canonicalText, '\u{FFFC}\n');
      final o = s.blocks.single.object!;
      expect(
        (o.kind, o.href, o.intrinsicWidth),
        (ObjectKind.image, 'OEBPS/i.png', null),
      );
      expect(s.diagnostics, isEmpty);
      expect(checkSection(s), isNull);
    });

    test('fetch null: como missing', () async {
      final c = await _provider({});
      final s = await _read(c, _item(_path));
      expect(s.blocks.single.placeholder, PlaceholderReason.missing);
      expect(_codes(s), ['resourceMissing']);
    });
  });

  group('§10.2 caminho XHTML', () {
    test('as três etapas dão a mesma Section que readSection', () async {
      final files = {
        _path: goldenDoc(
          '<p class="x">a &xyz;</p>',
          head: '<link rel="stylesheet" href="s.css"/>',
        ),
        'OEBPS/Text/s.css': '.x { font-weight: bold }',
      };
      final c = await _provider(files);
      final whole = await _read(c, _item(_path));
      final sink = DiagnosticSink();
      final source = await fetchSection(
        c,
        _item(_path),
        sink: sink,
        containerSink: DiagnosticSink(),
      );
      expect(source, isA<SectionBytes>());
      final bytes = source as SectionBytes;
      expect(utf8.decode(bytes.bytes), files[_path]);
      final prepared = await prepareSection(
        bytes,
        c,
        sheetCache: StyleSheetCache(),
        sink: sink,
        containerSink: DiagnosticSink(),
      );
      // A chave do cache existe antes da cascata.
      expect(prepared.sheets.cacheKey.single.path, 'OEBPS/Text/s.css');
      expect(prepared.viaXml, isTrue);
      expect(prepared.entities.unknownCount, 1);
      final into = SectionResult();
      for (final _ in buildPrepared(prepared, sink: sink, into: into)) {}
      expect(into.section, whole);
      expect(whole.blocks.single.runs.first.attrs, InlineAttr.strong);
      expect(_codes(whole), ['unknownEntity']);
      expect(whole.diagnostics.single.details, {
        'sample': 'xyz',
        'discarded': 1,
        'count': 1,
      });
      expect(() => SectionResult().section, throwsStateError);
    });

    test(
      'text/html vai ao tolerante sem xhtmlMalformed; XML quebrado com',
      () async {
        final c = await _provider({
          _path: '<html><body><P>a<P>b</body></html>',
          'OEBPS/q.xhtml': goldenDoc('<p>a</b>'),
        });
        final html = await _read(c, _item(_path, mediaType: 'text/html'));
        expect(html.canonicalText, 'a\nb\n');
        expect(html.diagnostics, isEmpty);
        final broken = await _read(c, _item('OEBPS/q.xhtml'));
        expect(broken.canonicalText, 'a\n');
        expect(_codes(broken), ['xhtmlMalformed']);
      },
    );

    test('agregados no fim: sectionTruncated junta os motivos (#50); anchorDuplicate', () async {
      final attrs = [for (var i = 0; i < 1025; i++) 'a$i="x"'].join(' ');
      final c = await _provider({
        _path: goldenDoc(
          '<p id="d" $attrs>a</p><p id="d">b</p>${'<p>x</p>' * maxSectionBlocks}',
        ),
      });
      final s = await _read(c, _item(_path));
      final t = s.diagnostics.singleWhere(
        (d) => identical(d.code, EpubDiagnosticCode.sectionTruncated),
      );
      expect(t.severity, EpubSeverity.warning);
      expect(
        (t.details['reasons'], t.details['limits']),
        ('attributes,blocks', '1024,200000'),
      );
      final a = s.diagnostics.singleWhere(
        (d) => identical(d.code, EpubDiagnosticCode.anchorDuplicate),
      );
      expect((a.details['sample'], a.details['discarded']), ('d', 1));
      expect(checkSection(s), isNull);
    }, timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 2)));
  });

  group('teto de bytes (#34)', () {
    Uint8List doc(int total, String tail) {
      final head = utf8.encode(goldenDoc('<p>'));
      final end = utf8.encode('$tail</p></body></html>');
      final fill = total - head.length - end.length;
      return Uint8List.fromList([
        ...head.sublist(0, head.length - '</body></html>'.length),
        ...List<int>.filled(fill + '</body></html>'.length, 0x61),
        ...end,
      ]);
    }

    test(
      '16 MiB passa; 16 MiB + 1 vira placeholder com só 8 MiB lidos',
      () async {
        final exact = doc(maxSectionBytes, 'FIM');
        expect(exact.length, maxSectionBytes);
        final c1 = await _provider({_path: exact});
        final source = await fetchSection(
          c1,
          _item(_path),
          sink: DiagnosticSink(),
          containerSink: DiagnosticSink(),
        );
        expect(source, isA<SectionBytes>());

        final over = doc(maxSectionBytes + 1, 'FIM');
        final c2 = await _provider({_path: over});
        final watch = Stopwatch()..start();
        final s = await _read(c2, _item(_path));
        expect(watch.elapsed, lessThan(const Duration(seconds: 5)));
        final d = s.diagnostics.single;
        expect(d.code, EpubDiagnosticCode.sectionTooLarge);
        expect(d.details, {
          'reason': 'bytes',
          'limit': maxSectionBytes,
          'size': maxSectionBytes + 1,
          'count': 1,
        });
        expect(
          s.blocks.every((b) => b.placeholder == PlaceholderReason.tooLarge),
          isTrue,
        );
        expect(
          s.canonicalText.contains('FIM'),
          isFalse,
          reason: 'depois dos 8 MiB',
        );
        expect(s.totalChars, lessThanOrEqualTo(maxSectionTextLength));
        expect(checkSection(s), isNull);
      },
      timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 2)),
    );
  });

  group('§10.3 falhas e strict', () {
    test(
      'fetch que lança: resourceUnreadable e placeholder unreadable',
      () async {
        final c = await _provider({_path: 'x'}, failingRead: {_path});
        final s = await _read(c, _item(_path));
        expect(s.blocks.single.placeholder, PlaceholderReason.unreadable);
        final d = s.diagnostics.single;
        expect(d.code, EpubDiagnosticCode.resourceUnreadable);
        expect(d.details['reason'], 'container');
        expect(
          (d.details['exception']! as String).length,
          lessThanOrEqualTo(64),
        );
      },
    );

    test('CRC errado com o contêiner em strict: a exceção dele propaga por identidade', () async {
      final zip = patchCentralU32(
        epubZip({
          'META-INF/container.xml': utf8.encode('<x/>'),
          _path: utf8.encode(goldenDoc('<p>a</p>')),
        }),
        2,
        cdCrc,
        0x12345678,
      );
      final containerSink = DiagnosticSink(strict: true);
      final c = await ZipContainer.open(
        MemoryEpubByteSource(zip),
        sink: containerSink,
      );
      Object? thrown;
      try {
        await _read(c, _item(_path), containerSink: containerSink);
      } on EpubException catch (e) {
        thrown = e;
      }
      expect(thrown, isNotNull);
      expect(identical(thrown, containerSink.lastStrictException), isTrue);
      // Sem strict no contêiner: a seção sai, com o CRC no sink dele.
      final relaxed = DiagnosticSink();
      final c2 = await ZipContainer.open(
        MemoryEpubByteSource(zip),
        sink: relaxed,
      );
      final s = await _read(c2, _item(_path), containerSink: relaxed);
      expect(s.canonicalText, 'a\n');
      expect(
        relaxed.diagnostics.single.code,
        EpubDiagnosticCode.zipCrcMismatch,
      );
    });

    test('strict na seção: warning da IR lança EpubSectionParseException; info não', () async {
      final c = await _provider({
        _path: goldenDoc('<p>&xyz;</p><p id="a"/><p id="a">x</p>'),
      });
      final ok = await _read(
        c,
        _item(_path),
        sink: DiagnosticSink(strict: true),
      );
      expect(_codes(ok), ['unknownEntity', 'anchorDuplicate']);
      final strict = DiagnosticSink(strict: true);
      await expectLater(
        _read(c, _item(_path, missing: true), sink: strict),
        throwsA(
          isA<EpubSectionParseException>().having(
            (e) => e.message,
            'message',
            startsWith('resourceMissing: '),
          ),
        ),
      );
      expect(strict.lastStrictException, isA<EpubSectionParseException>());
    });

    test(
      'onCheckpoint é chamado; a exceção dele propaga e não vira placeholder',
      () async {
        final c = await _provider({_path: goldenDoc('<p>a</p><p>b</p>')});
        var calls = 0;
        await _read(c, _item(_path), onCheckpoint: () async => calls++);
        expect(calls, greaterThan(2));
        final cancel = EpubContainerException('cancelado');
        await expectLater(
          _read(c, _item(_path), onCheckpoint: () async => throw cancel),
          throwsA(same(cancel)),
        );
      },
    );

    test('Section.diagnostics é o sink da seção', () async {
      final c = await _provider({_path: goldenDoc('<p>&xyz;</p>')});
      final sink = DiagnosticSink();
      final s = await _read(c, _item(_path), sink: sink);
      expect(s.diagnostics.length, sink.diagnostics.length);
      expect(
        identical(s.diagnostics.single.code, sink.diagnostics.single.code),
        isTrue,
      );
    });
  });

  group('foco de revisão', () {
    test(
      'XHTML vazio ou só <html/>: Section vazia e válida, sem exceção',
      () async {
        final c = await _provider({_path: '', 'OEBPS/h.xhtml': '<html/>'});
        final empty = await _read(c, _item(_path));
        expect(empty.canonicalText, '');
        expect(empty.blocks, isEmpty);
        expect(_codes(empty), ['xhtmlMalformed']);
        expect(checkSection(empty), isNull);
        final bare = await _read(c, _item('OEBPS/h.xhtml'));
        expect(bare.canonicalText, '');
        expect(bare.diagnostics, isEmpty);
        expect(checkSection(bare), isNull);
      },
    );
  });

  test('constantes', () {
    expect(maxSectionBytes, 16 * 1024 * 1024);
    expect(maxPlaceholderSourceBytes, 8 * 1024 * 1024);
  });
}
```
